Minimum description length, algorithmic complexity, proper scoring rules, varia-tional inference, and Landauer's principle motivate measurement choices but do not entail theformula. CPR-IE is a constructed efficiency representation, not a universal law or a definition ofintelligence itself.

</details>


### [193] [Long Horizon Transformer Quantile Fault Prediction for Multi Site Industrial Predictive Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04840)
*David J Poland,Daniele Ravi,Na Helian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TQRNN30d框架，将双阶段分位数回归神经网络(QRNN)特征提取器与多流时序融合分类器相结合，用于30天时间跨度的长时域预测性维护任务。该系统将81通道机器行为逐小时映射为324维分位数状态表示，720个有序小时词组成30天文档输入模型。在72台机器、9个制造工厂的机器不重叠划分评估中，TQRNN30d在30天达到79.97% F1，并在7/14/30天固定阈值比较中领先全部18个基线模型。但结果表明仅支持同构九厂机队内的留出机器性能，未建立跨场地、跨设备或跨行业泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域预测性维护需要在以天而非小时计的规划窗口内区分缓慢演变的退化与正常工况变化，这对现有模型构成挑战。论文的核心动机是探究显式的条件分位数表示能否为这一长时域分类问题提供信息丰富且有效的分类器接口。

Method: TQRNN30d框架由两部分组成：(1)双阶段QRNN特征提取器，将81通道逐小时机器行为编码为324维分位数状态表示；(2)多流时序融合分类器，融合分位数状态、动态协变量、通道级静态元数据及168小时潜在历史流，采用门控残差处理、因果循环编码和元数据条件跨模态注意力。此外，基于单步前向预测误差发散的有界不稳定性感知信号在最长视界提供辅助记忆调制。评估采用43/14/15的训练/验证/测试机器不重叠划分，覆盖9个制造设施的72台机器。

Result: 在30天视界，TQRNN30d取得79.97% F1、80.18%召回率、81.82%精确率、82.39%准确率和0.820 ROC-AUC。在18个基线中，TQRNN30d在7天、14天和30天固定阈值比较中全面领先，14天视界F1优势最大。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持在观测的同构九厂机队内对留出机器具有良好的性能表现，但泛化结论受限——未验证跨场地、跨设备类型或跨行业的迁移能力，这为后续研究留下了开放性边界。

Abstract: Long-horizon predictive maintenance requires models to distinguish slowly evolving degradation from normal operating-regime variation over planning windows measured in days rather than hours. This paper evaluates whether an explicit conditional-quantile representation provides an informative classifier interface for this problem. The proposed TQRNN30d framework combines a dual-stage quantile regression neural network (QRNN) feature extractor with a multi-stream temporal fusion classifier. Each hourly word of 81-channel machine behaviour is mapped to a 324-dimensional quantile-state representation, and 720 ordered hourly words form the 30-day document supplied to the long-horizon model. The classifier fuses quantile states with dynamic covariates, channel-level static metadata, and a 168-hour latent-history stream using gated residual processing, causal recurrent encoding, and metadata-conditioned cross-modal attention. A bounded instability-aware signal derived from sustained one-word-ahead prediction-error divergence provides auxiliary memory modulation at the longest horizon. Evaluation uses a machine-disjoint 43/14/15 train/validation/test allocation across 72 machines in nine manufacturing facilities. At 30 days, TQRNN30d achieves 79.97% F1, 80.18% recall, 81.82% precision, 82.39% accuracy, and 0.820 ROC-AUC. It leads all 18 evaluated baselines at the 7-, 14-, and 30-day fixed-threshold comparisons, with the largest F1 advantage at 14 days. The results support held-out-machine performance within the observed homogeneous nine-facility fleet, but do not establish unseen-site, cross-equipment, or cross-sector generalisation.

</details>


### [194] [ElderBench: Benchmarking Autonomous Mobile Agents for Older Adults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04850)
*Weide Zhan,Qumu Shaqu,Yuanqing Liu,Peng Zhang,Jiahao Liu,Kam Him Lam,Ning Gu,Zhan Hu,Tun Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ElderBench，这是首个针对老年人在真实场景中使用智能手机的移动GUI智能体评测基准，基于249个从老年人中收集的自然任务构建，系统揭示了老年人语言特征（如间接表达、指代模糊、请求不明确）与现有基准指令的差异，并发现主流GUI智能体在老年人导向指令下性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GUI基准主要基于明确的目标导向指令，未能捕捉老年用户自然语言中常见的间接言语、指代歧义和未充分指定的请求。这种指令与现实老年人交互模式之间的不匹配可能阻碍智能体的可靠部署，因此需要一个反映真实老年人语言模式的评测基准。

Method: 从20个应用中收集249个由老年人自然引发的智能手机任务构建ElderBench，从句法、语义和语用三个维度刻画老年人指令与现有GUI基准指令的语言差异；在在线和离线两种设置下评估主流GUI智能体和视觉语言模型；通过受控指令标准化、失败分析和细粒度语言特征分析，识别老年人特定语言模式如何导致智能体失败。

Result: 主流GUI智能体和VLM在处理老年人导向指令时展现出显著的性能退化；老年人特有的语言模式（间接表达、歧义、不完整请求等）是导致智能体失败的关键因素。

Conclusion: 研究为开发更具适应性、可解释性和包容老年人的GUI智能体提供了可操作的设计洞见，强调了在基准设计中纳入真实用户语言模式的重要性。

Abstract: While autonomous mobile agents hold great potential for assisting older adults with smartphone usage, existing GUI benchmarks mainly rely on explicit, goal-oriented instructions and rarely capture the naturally occurring language patterns of older users, such as indirect speech, referential ambiguity, and under-specified requests. This mismatch between benchmark instructions and real-world elderly interactions may hinder reliable agent deployment. To address this gap, we present ElderBench, the first benchmark for evaluating mobile GUI agents in authentic elderly-oriented scenarios. ElderBench is constructed from 249 naturally elicited smartphone tasks collected from older adults across 20 applications. We first characterize the linguistic divergence between elderly instructions and existing GUI benchmark instructions from syntactic, semantic, and pragmatic perspectives. We then evaluate mainstream GUI agents and Vision-Language Models under both online and offline settings, revealing substantial performance degradation when handling elderly-oriented instructions. Through controlled instruction normalization, failure analysis, and fine-grained linguistic feature analysis, we further identify how elderly-specific language patterns contribute to agent failures. Our findings provide actionable design insights toward more adaptive, interpretable, and age-inclusive GUI agents for older adults.

</details>


### [195] [MM-IFEval-Pro: A Multilingual and Attack-Resistant Benchmark for Instruction-Following in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04859)
*Changming Xiao,Zhenliang Ni,Jinhui He,Han Shu,Jie Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了MM-IFEval-Pro，一个覆盖中英文和指令劫持场景的多模态指令跟随基准，包含4大任务类别、24个子类别以及8个指令类别、52个子类别，平均每个样本含3.0个约束；同时构建了基于强化学习的中文与对抗性指令训练集，显著提升模型在MM-IFEval-Pro上的表现，并能有效迁移到其他主流多模态基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在多模态指令跟随评估方面存在两个主要缺陷：一是语言覆盖范围有限（主要聚焦英文），二是缺乏足够的对抗性安全场景（如指令劫持），导致无法充分评估模型在多语言和真实安全敏感场景下的实际能力。

Method: 提出了包含中英双语及多样化指令劫持案例的多模态指令跟随基准MM-IFEval-Pro；基准涵盖4大任务类别和24个子类别，以及8个指令类别和52个子类别，每个样本平均包含3.0个约束以模拟复杂指令场景；并构建了融合中文与对抗性指令的强化学习训练集。

Result: 该强化学习训练集显著提升了模型在MM-IFEval-Pro上的性能，且迁移效果良好，在其他主流多模态基准上也表现出一致的性能提升，展现了跨任务和跨语言的强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过引入多语言覆盖和对抗性安全场景，MM-IFEval-Pro及其配套的训练策略有效提升了VLM在复杂多语言指令跟随和安全性方面的能力，且具有跨任务、跨语言的强泛化性，为多模态指令跟随评估提供了更全面的基准。

Abstract: As vision-language models (VLMs) rapidly advance in image understanding, cross-modal reasoning, and complex instruction execution, instruction-following capability has become a key indicator of their reliability and practicality. However, existing multimodal instruction-following benchmarks still suffer from limited language coverage and insufficient adversarial safety scenarios, making them inadequate for evaluating real-world multilingual and safety-sensitive settings. To address these gaps, we present MM-IFEval-Pro, a multimodal instruction-following benchmark covering Chinese and English tasks as well as diverse instruction hijacking cases. MM-IFEval-Pro includes 4 major task categories and 24 subcategories and 8 instruction categories with 52 subcategories, with each sample containing an average of 3.0 constraints to realistically simulate complex instruction scenarios. We further construct a reinforcement-learning training set enriched with Chinese and adversarial instructions, which significantly improves model performance on MM-IFEval-Pro and transfers effectively to other mainstream multimodal benchmarks, demonstrating strong cross-task and cross-language generalization.

</details>


### [196] [MZ-Rain: Moisture-Budget-Guided Zero-Inflated Model for Station-Level Precipitation Nowcasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04864)
*Yifang Zhang,Shengwu Xiong,Henan Wang,Wenjie Yin,Yuqiang Zhang,Chen Zhou,Hua Chen,Qile Zhao,Pengfei Duan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MZ-Rain是一个基于水分收支方程引导的零膨胀sLSTM框架，用于站点级降水临近预报。它通过分解降水形成过程为特定物理路径，并采用自适应Tweedie建模策略处理零膨胀数据，在多个评价指标上优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 站点级降水临近预报面临两大挑战：(1)传统时间序列模型缺乏物理引导，将气象变量视为同质集合，忽略了它们在降水形成中的不同作用，导致预测偏离物理过程；(2)降水数据严重零膨胀，干期主导数据集，掩盖了有意义的降水模式，给建模带来困难。

Method: 提出MZ-Rain框架：由水分收支方程引导，将降水形成过程分解为水分储存、水分输送、地表蒸发和降水持续性四个过程特定路径，分别由专门的sLSTM分支捕获时序演化；采用自适应Tweedie建模策略，自适应调节降雨均值并联合学习降水发生作为辅助任务，平衡干湿判别与定量降水估计。

Result: 在跨不同地理和气候区域的广泛实验中，MZ-Rain在CSI、FAR、MSE和MAE等多个评价指标上持续优于强基线，尤其在强降水事件预报中表现出卓越技能，并受益于物理基础的过程建模。

Conclusion: MZ-Rain通过物理引导的过程分解和针对零膨胀的自适应Tweedie建模，有效解决了站点级降水临近预报中的物理缺失和数据不平衡问题，在强降水预测中展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Accurate station-level precipitation nowcasting is critical for agriculture, water resource management, and disaster prevention, which typically is formulated as a time series forecasting problem. However, conventional time-series modeling techniques face two major challenges in addressing station-level precipitation nowcasting: (1) Lack of Physics-Guided Modeling}, where meteorological variables are treated as a homogeneous set without accounting for their distinct roles in precipitation formation, leads to predictions that deviate from the physical processes governing precipitation. (2) Severe zero inflation in precipitation, where dry intervals dominate the dataset, obscuring meaningful precipitation patterns and complicating the predictive modeling. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{MZ-Rain}, a moisture-budget-guided zero-inflated sLSTM framework for station-level precipitation nowcasting. Guided by the moisture budget equation, MZ-Rain decomposes the precipitation formation process into process-specific pathways corresponding to moisture storage, moisture transport, surface evaporation, and precipitation persistence, and captures their temporal evolution through dedicated sLSTM branches. To account for the zero-inflated nature of precipitation, MZ-Rain introduces an adaptive Tweedie modeling strategy that adaptively modulates the rainfall mean while jointly learning precipitation occurrence as an auxiliary task, enabling the model to better balance dry-wet discrimination and quantitative precipitation estimation. Extensive experiments across diverse geographical and climatic regimes demonstrate that MZ-Rain consistently outperforms strong baselines on multiple evaluation metrics, including CSI, FAR, MSE, and MAE. In particular, the model exhibits superior skill in forecasting heavy precipitation events, while benefiting from physically grounded process modeling.

</details>


### [197] [CoSkill: Joint Reinforcement Learning of Reasoning and Meta-Skill Agents for Hierarchical Skill Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04865)
*Jinyuan Feng,Dongmin Li,Yiqun Chen,Yang Gao,Xing Chen,Huimu Wang,Zhiqiang Pu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CoSkill，一个统一的多智能体强化学习框架，将静态的元技能工作流重构为可学习的元技能智能体，并与推理智能体共享单一主干进行联合训练，实现端到端的协同适应。在ALFWorld和WebShop上分别达到98.4%和90.6%的成功率，显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能库范式存在结构性缺陷：要么将技能进化与策略优化解耦，要么将元技能实例化为固定工作流。这些方法都将技能视为被动管理的对象，限制了技能的灵活进化及其与推理智能体的协同适应，无法充分提升智能体强化学习的样本效率。

Method: CoSkill将静态的元技能工作流重新定义为可学习的Meta-Skill Agent，并使其与Reasoning Agent在分层技能库上共享单一骨干网络进行联合训练。推理智能体基于检索到的任务技能及其子集中的步技能来条件化其动作，而其任务表现则指导元技能智能体精炼这些步技能，形成双向协同适应机制。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop上，CoSkill分别达到98.4%和90.6%的成功率，相比现有基线提升3.5和6.2个百分点。同时展现出优越的早期样本效率、渐近性能和墙钟时间效率。

Conclusion: 将技能建模为与推理智能体共同进化的学习对象而非固定工作流，能够实现更好的协同适应，从而显著提升LLM智能体在具身任务和网页导航任务上的表现。

Abstract: Skill libraries improve the sample efficiency of agentic reinforcement learning (RL) by enabling large language model (LLM) agents to reuse procedural knowledge. Yet existing paradigms exhibit structural shortcomings: they either decouple skill evolution from policy optimization or instantiate meta-skills as fixed workflows. Both treat skills as passive objects to be managed, limiting the flexible evolution of skills and their co-adaptation with the reasoning agent. To address the limitations, we propose CoSkill, a unified multi-agent RL framework that recasts the static meta-skill workflow as a learnable Meta-Skill Agent and jointly trains it with a Reasoning Agent over a hierarchical skill library. By modeling the Reasoning and Meta-Skill Agents as a cooperative team sharing a single backbone, CoSkill enables end-to-end co-adaptation: the Reasoning Agent conditions its actions on a retrieved task skill and step skills selected from its child set, while its task performance guides the Meta-Skill Agent in refining those step skills. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop show that CoSkill substantially outperforms prior skill-based and RL baselines, achieving success rates of 98.4% and 90.6%, respectively (+3.5 and +6.2 pp). As shown in Figure 1, CoSkill achieves superior early-stage sample efficiency, asymptotic performance, and wall-clock efficiency. Our code is available at https://github.com/jinyuan-cookie/CoSkill.

</details>


### [198] [LLM-Assisted Behavioural and Scenario Augmentation for Agent-Based Energy Adoption Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04866)
*Iias Faiud,Hossein Khaleghy,Michael Schukat,Karl Mason*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个混合框架，将LLM辅助的规格设计（包含有界行为量规和结构化场景规格）集成到校准的基于主体的模型（ABM）中，用于模拟爱尔兰奶牛场的太阳能光伏采纳行为，在保持原有技术经济采纳机制的同时引入可解释的行为调节和情景驱动的不确定性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）的发展为基于仿真的能源政策分析提供了新的机会，尤其是支持结构化行为假设和探索性技术经济场景。然而，直接用LLM推理替代采纳模型会引起关于可解释性、可复现性和行为有效性的担忧，因此需要一种受控的混合方法来兼顾LLM的表达能力与传统ABM的严谨性。

Method: 提出一个混合框架，包括两个关键组件：（1）有界行为量规，将行为效应表示为可解释的保守、平衡和乐观三类量规；（2）规则验证的结构化场景规格，用于探索未来的政策与市场条件。框架在保留原始技术经济采纳机制的基础上，通过有界行为调制和场景驱动的不确定性分析对其进行增强，并在校准的太阳能光伏采纳ABM中实现。

Result: 在多种政策设置、蒙特卡洛世界和随机种子下进行实验，结果表明模型行为稳定且经济上合理，采纳结果在不同行为制度下保持有界和单调。该框架相较于对应的逻辑斯蒂（logistic）情形可实现最高约13%的行为采纳增长，且未产生不稳定的或不现实的饱和动态。

Conclusion: LLM辅助的规格设计能够以受控、可复现且与政策相关的方式集成到校准的能源ABM中，为能源政策分析提供了一种兼顾LLM灵活性与传统模型严谨性的可行路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) create opportunities to enrich simulation-based energy policy analysis, particularly by supporting structured behavioural assumptions and exploratory techno-economic scenarios. However, directly replacing adoption models with LLM reasoning raises concerns regarding interpretability, reproducibility, and behavioural validity. This paper proposes a hybrid framework for LLM-assisted specification design, integrating bounded behavioural rubrics and structured scenario specifications into a calibrated agent-based model (ABM) of solar photovoltaic (PV) adoption by Irish dairy farms. The proposed approach preserves the original techno-economic adoption mechanism while augmenting it with bounded behavioural modulation and scenario-driven uncertainty analysis. Behavioural effects are represented through interpretable conservative, balanced, and optimistic rubrics, while future policy and market conditions are explored through fixed, rule-validated scenario specifications. Experimental results across multiple policy settings, Monte Carlo worlds, and random seeds demonstrate stable and economically plausible behaviour, with adoption outcomes remaining bounded and monotonic across behavioural regimes. The framework achieves up to approximately 13% behavioural adoption increase relative to the corresponding logistic case without producing unstable or unrealistic saturation dynamics. The results demonstrate that LLM-assisted specifications can be integrated into calibrated energy ABMs in a controlled, reproducible, and policy-relevant manner.

</details>


### [199] [From Interaction Traces to Persistent Skills: Online Evolution for Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04869)
*Longtao Hu,Xiao Liang,Linchao Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个在线技能演化框架（Skill-Evo4GUI），将计算机使用智能体的交互轨迹与评估者反馈转化为持久化、版本化的可复用过程库。通过与空库对照实验，在OSWorld四个应用域中验证了演化库能提升固定计算机使用栈的得分（提高5.7至18.6个百分点），但收益是有条件的，重复修订并不保证恢复原任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体在图形界面中执行任务的交互经验通常是瞬态的，程序性知识无法从一次回滚中系统性地留存、精炼并在后续任务中复用。虽然已有技能库提供外部程序性知识，但其相对于无技能库智能体的增量价值以及在重复交互下的纵向动态尚未被充分刻画。

Method: 提出在线技能演化框架：将交互轨迹和评估者反馈转化为持久化、版本化的可复用过程库。每次迭代在冻结的库快照上执行，证据引导的技能更新在后续迭代中生效而不改变模型参数。在OSWorld的四个应用域中，将完整演化库系统与配置匹配的空库对照组在相同动作生成、GUI接地栈、任务集和迭代范围下进行对比。

Result: 经过五轮空库热身后，完整系统在后热身期所有四个域的平均评估得分均更高，平均差异为5.7至18.6个百分点，且随时间呈现出域相关的稳定性。在GIMP中，溯源分析揭示了跨任务来源边界的检索以及修订反复（反复的已接受编辑未能恢复源任务）。

Conclusion: 演化技能库可以被视为可审计的、共享的程序性记忆，能提升固定的计算机使用栈的性能；但其收益是有条件的，重复修订并不保证任务的恢复。代码已开源。

Abstract: Computer-use agents can execute increasingly complex tasks in graphical interfaces, but their interaction experience is typically transient: procedural knowledge acquired from one rollout is not systematically retained, refined, and reused in later tasks. Existing skill libraries provide external procedural knowledge, yet their incremental value over the same agent operating without skills, as well as their longitudinal dynamics under repeated interaction, remain insufficiently characterized. We present an online skill-evolution framework that converts interaction trajectories and evaluator feedback into a persistent, versioned library of reusable procedures. Each iteration executes against a frozen library snapshot, and evidence-guided skill updates become available in subsequent iterations without changing model parameters. We compare the full evolving-library system with a configuration-matched empty-library control across four OSWorld application domains under the same fixed action-generation and GUI-grounding stack, task sets, and iteration horizons. Following a five-iteration empty-library warm-up, Full attains a higher post-warm-up mean evaluator score in all four observed domain runs, with mean differences ranging from 5.7 to 18.6 percentage points and domain-dependent temporal stability. In GIMP, provenance-aware analysis reveals retrieval across task-of-origin boundaries and revision churn, where repeated accepted edits fail to recover the originating task. These findings characterize evolving skill libraries as auditable, shared procedural memory that can improve a fixed computer-use stack, while showing that their benefits are conditional and repeated revision does not guarantee recovery. Code is released at https://github.com/LongtaoHu/Skill-Evo4GUI.

</details>


### [200] [CHAMP: Cross-domain Hybrid Architecture for Matchmaking and Prediction in Online Multi-Player Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04870)
*Kai Wang,Ge Fan,Chaoyun Zhang,Yuyang Jiang,Yuze Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CHAMP，一个跨域匹配框架，解决了MOBA游戏中匹配系统在实际大规模部署中的三大瓶颈：冷启动、分布不一致和数据稀疏。核心是DAWN网络（Domain-Aware Win-rate Network），通过混合域特征提取和域感知编码器实现单模型多模式服务。离线预测准确率达67.73%，在线A/B测试在从新手到顶尖玩家的全联盟天梯中一致降低了不平衡对局比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作CUPID将匹配问题框架化为分配再优化问题，但实际部署面临三大瓶颈：大多数排队玩家缺乏足够的目标模式比赛历史（冷启动）、技能分布在不同段位间差异显著（分布不一致）、极端技能段位数据严重匮乏。这些瓶颈使得单一模式的胜率预测器难以在多样化玩家群体中有效工作。

Method: CHAMP框架的核心创新包括：(1) 混合域特征收集：用时间戳排序的跨模式短期序列替代仅目标模式的玩家档案，其切片标注目标域特征，并辅以每模式的长期、实时和团队统计；(2) DAWN网络：包含Domain-Aware Knowledge Extractor（DAKE）将目标模式属性编译为可学习表征，输入到域感知的时间/空间/置换OmniNet编码器（DATOE/DASOE/DAPOE）中，在单一共享网络内联合学习模式条件化表征与每模式去偏；(3) 在线部署时，一个训练好的DAWN服务所有支持的模式，仅以每模式的位置满意度阈值作为唯一模式特定参数。

Result: 离线实验中，DAWN实现67.73%的胜率预测准确率，优于所有评估的注意力与序列基线。在线A/B测试覆盖某大型MOBA游戏从新手到精英模式顶尖玩家的完整天梯，一致性地降低了不平衡对局比例。对低段位玩家，CHAMP将5分钟击杀碾压率降低了最高20.73%。

Conclusion: CHAMP框架通过跨域知识共享有效解决了匹配系统大规模部署中的数据稀疏、冷启动和分布不一致问题，实现了单一模型服务多模式的规模化匹配方案，并在实际大规模MOBA游戏中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Multiplayer Online Battle Arena (MOBA) games rely on matchmaking to maintain competitive balance. Our prior work, CUPID, framed matchmaking as an assignment re-optimization problem and showed that a single-mode win-rate predictor can meaningfully rebalance teams. However, deploying such a system across diverse player populations exposes three practical bottlenecks: most queueing players lack sufficient in-mode match history (cold start), skill distributions shift drastically across rank tiers (distribution inconsistency), and extreme skill segments are severely data-starved.
  We present CHAMP, a cross-domain matchmaking framework that resolves these deployment bottlenecks. To address data sparsity and cold starts, CHAMP replaces the target-mode-only player profile with a hybrid domain feature collection: a timestamp-ordered cross-mode short-term sequence whose slices are annotated with target-domain features, plus per-mode breakdowns of long-term, real-time and team statistics. We further propose the Domain-Aware Win-rate Network (DAWN): a Domain-aware Knowledge Extractor (DAKE) compiles target-mode attributes into learnable representations that feed Domain-Aware Temporal/Spatial/Permutation OmniNet Encoders (DATOE/DASOE/DAPOE), so that mode-conditioned representations and per-mode debiasing are learned jointly inside a single shared network. Online, one trained DAWN serves every supported mode, with per-mode position-satisfaction thresholds as the only mode-specific knob.
  Offline, DAWN achieves 67.73% win-rate prediction accuracy, outperforming all evaluated attention and sequence baselines. Online A/B tests across the entire League ladder of a large-scale MOBA game, from novice players up to the top-expert players served by Elite Mode, demonstrate consistent drops in imbalanced matches. For lower-tier players, CHAMP reduces the 5-minute kill crushing rate by up to 20.73%.

</details>


### [201] [AutoLR: Automating the Path from Research to Launch Review in Industrial Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04871)
*Qi Zhang,Yanlin Chen,Wenchao Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍AutoLR，一个NetEase游戏社区应用DASHEN中构建的自主研究到上线（research-to-launch）流水线系统。AutoLR将工业推荐系统的迭代过程自动化，结合多专家委员会辩论、确定性证据加权探索-利用选择器、以及分层知识系统，由LLM代理负责语义推理和代码生成，但确定性控制器保留对执行、指标提取、护栏和状态转换的权威。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统的改进是一个高度迭代的研究与工程过程，通常跨越数天的实验周期。虽然LLM可以辅助单个阶段，但整体流程仍然依赖于人的协调，缺乏一个能够在大规模、长时间运行中可靠协调各阶段的自动化框架。

Method: AutoLR结合三大系统机制：(1) 多专家委员会——对提案进行辩论和对抗性评审；(2) 确定性证据加权探索-利用选择器——在有限试验预算中分配候选方向并使用委员会重排序；(3) 分层知识系统——整合外部研究、生产系统知识和DASHEN领域知识（游戏社区、玩家特征、内容交互模式），并结合配置、补丁、日志、失败和离线结果的后验证据。LLM代理负责语义推理和代码生成，而确定性控制器掌控执行、指标提取、安全护栏和持久状态转换。

Result: 论文描述AutoLR从最初仅覆盖Auto Launch Review扩展到完整的research-to-launch自主流水线，向上游延伸至研究方向的识别和实验执行阶段，形成端到端的自主实验循环。

Conclusion: AutoLR展示了LLM代理与确定性控制器混合架构在工业推荐系统持续改进中的可行性——通过将语义推理工作交给LLM，同时由确定性机制保证执行安全性和流程可靠性，从而在长周期迭代过程中实现自动化。

Abstract: Improving an industrial recommender is an iterative research-and-engineering process rather than a direct path from idea to deployment. In \textbf{DASHEN, NetEase's gaming-community app}, algorithm engineers typically identify promising directions from research papers, technical reports, and prior production experiments; reproduce or adapt the underlying methods; implement them in the production codebase; and evaluate the resulting models through training and offline experiments. Promising candidates are then advanced to online A/B tests, and those demonstrating robust gains are submitted to Launch Review---the internal gate for full-traffic rollout. Large language models (LLMs) can assist with individual stages of this workflow, but the overall process remains human-dependent without a harness that can reliably coordinate them across long-running, often multi-day experimental cycles. We present \textbf{AutoLR}, initially built as \textbf{Auto Launch Review} and later extended upstream into an autonomous research-to-launch harness. AutoLR combines three system mechanisms: a \textbf{multi-expert council} that debates and adversarially reviews proposals; a \textbf{deterministic evidence-weighted exploration--exploitation selector} that allocates a limited trial budget across candidate directions and uses Council reranking; and a layered knowledge system that combines external research, production-system knowledge, and DASHEN-specific domain knowledge---such as game communities, player characteristics, and content-interaction patterns---with posterior evidence from configurations, patches, logs, failures, and offline outcomes. LLM agents perform semantic reasoning and code generation, while deterministic controllers retain authority over execution, metric extraction, guardrails, and persistent state transitions.

</details>


### [202] [MARLA: A Conceptual Scaffold for Regulatory Learning under the EU AI Act](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04877)
*Alessio Buscemi,Tom Deckenbrunnen,Imane Hmiddou,Marco Billi,Livio Rubino,Silvia Rizzuto Ferruzza,Daniele Pagani,Antonino Rotolo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MARLA（Map, Assess, Report, Learn, Adapt）概念框架，将《欧盟人工智能法案》的监管学习组织为五阶段循环，从地方、国家到欧洲三层治理架构中实施法律要求，并为技术和法律利益相关者提供共享词汇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟AI法案将监管定位为安全、可信和可市场创新的一部分，但其实施过程中产生的证据需要在治理和法律知识之间转化。然而，证据生产者（技术方）和依赖者（法律/监管方）处于不同的专业领域，缺乏共同的概念框架来组织监管学习，影响了监管的一致解释、有效监督和适应性演变。

Method: 提出MARLA五阶段概念支架（Map映射、Assess评估、Report报告、Learn学习、Adapt适应），将法律要求实施到社会技术实践的循环过程，定位于AI法案治理架构的地方、国家和欧洲三个层面。该框架刻意非规定性，前三个阶段各自生成可文档化的监管学习形式。通过两个试点案例研究和一项前瞻性国家到欧洲的说明进行验证。

Result: 通过两个试点案例和一项前瞻性展示，证明了MARLA框架能为技术和法律利益相关者提供共享词汇，支持将实施证据系统性地转化为可文档化的监管学习成果，从而促进AI法案在多层治理架构中的一致解释与有效监督。

Conclusion: MARLA框架为非规定性的监管学习提供了概念性组织工具，弥合了技术实践与法律治理之间的专业鸿沟，有助于实现欧盟AI法案所追求的安全、可信且市场就绪创新的监管基础设施愿景。

Abstract: The EU AI Act positions regulation as part of the infrastructure for safe, trustworthy and market-ready innovation. Realising this ambition requires regulatory learning: the evidence generated during implementation must be translated into governance and legal knowledge that supports consistent interpretation, effective oversight, and adaptation as technologies evolve. Yet the actors who produce this evidence and those who rely on it operate in different professional worlds. This paper proposes MARLA (Map, Assess, Report, Learn, Adapt), a conceptual scaffold organising regulatory learning as a five-stage cycle centred on the implementation of legal requirements into socio-technical practices, situated at the Local, National and European levels of the AI Act's governance architecture. Deliberately non-prescriptive, MARLA gives technical and legal stakeholders a shared vocabulary in which each of the first three stages generates its own documentable form of regulatory learning. We illustrate the scaffold with two piloted case studies and a prospective National-to-European illustration.

</details>


### [203] [Reinforcement Learning for Sequential Solar PV Policy Design under Uncertainty: An Agent-Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04880)
*Iias Faiud,Jonaid Shianifar,Michael Schukat,Karl Mason*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究将光伏政策设计建模为序贯决策问题，通过强化学习（PPO、SAC、TD3）与随机智能体模型（ABM）的集成，在16年时间跨度上学习年度激励政策（资本补助、贷款贴息、上网电价）。在成本-采纳权重的标量化奖励框架下探索政策偏好变化，得到的权衡结构清晰：高采纳政策（TD3）实现约4,145户采纳、成本4,173万欧元；低成本政策（PPO）仅需727万欧元但采纳降至2,682户；平衡政策（PPO）以2,247万欧元实现3,495户采纳。多种算法下权衡模式一致，表明采纳-成本关系的稳健性，且RL框架相比静态基线能探索更广泛的策略空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计既有效又在财政上可持续的光伏补贴政策，需要在不确定性环境下平衡采纳增益与公共支出，同时考虑异质性决策行为。传统静态政策设计难以应对这种复杂权衡，需要一种能自适应学习的政策优化方法。

Method: 将光伏政策设计形式化为序贯决策问题，将强化学习（PPO、SAC、TD3三种算法）与随机智能体模型（ABM）集成。政策制定者智能体在16年周期内每年选择资本补助、补贴贷款利率和上网电价等激励工具，并通过标量化奖励框架中的成本权重参数来探索采纳-成本的权衡结构。

Result: 产生了清晰的权衡结构：最高采纳策略（TD3, 成本权重=0.5）实现约4,145户采纳、成本4,173万欧元；最低成本策略（PPO, 成本权重=2.0）将支出降至727万欧元但仅获得2,682户采纳；平衡策略（PPO, 成本权重=1.6）以2,247万欧元实现3,495户采纳。不同算法间呈现一致的权衡模式，且RL框架相比静态基线策略能探索更广泛的策略配置。

Conclusion: 研究成果证明了强化学习作为灵活工具在不确定性环境下进行自适应政策设计的潜力。采纳-成本权衡关系的跨算法一致性验证了该方法的稳健性，为光伏政策制定提供了可操作的数据驱动框架。

Abstract: Designing effective and fiscally sustainable policies for solar photovoltaic (PV) adoption requires balancing adoption gains against public expenditure under uncertainty and heterogeneous decision-making. This study formulates PV policy design as a sequential decision problem and integrates reinforcement learning (RL) with a stochastic agent-based model (ABM) that simulates yearly solar PV adoption under uncertainty. A policymaker agent selects annual incentives, including capital grants, subsidised loan rates, and feed-in tariffs, over a 16-year horizon. Adoption--cost trade-offs are explored by varying policy preferences within a scalarised reward framework. Policies are learned using PPO, SAC, and TD3 and evaluated under stochastic simulation. The results show that this approach produces a clear trade-off structure: the highest-adoption policy (TD3, $w_{\text{cost}}=0.5$) achieves approximately 4,145 adopters at a cost of EUR 41.73 million, while the lowest-cost policy (PPO, $w_{\text{cost}}=2.0$) reduces expenditure to EUR 7.27 million with 2,682 adopters. The balanced policy (PPO, $w_{\text{cost}}=1.6$) achieves 3,495 adopters at a cost of EUR 22.47 million. Across algorithms, consistent trade-off patterns are observed, indicating robustness of the adoption--cost relationship. Compared with static baseline policies, the RL framework explores a broader range of policy configurations. These findings demonstrate the potential of RL as a flexible tool for adaptive policy design under uncertainty.

</details>


### [204] [From Language Models to World-Acting Systems: Progress and Limits of Agentic AI across Digital, Social, Virtual, and Physical Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04894)
*Linsen Zhu,Mengqing Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对截至2026年8月31日的大型语言模型作为智能体（agent）的研究进行批判性综述，提出以"合理授权"（justified delegation）作为分析框架，主张区分模型能力、系统集成、持久性与安全授权，而不是将智能体进展简单地叙述为单一走向自主性的进程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型通过与外部系统的交互成为具有因果效力的智能体（工具调用、界面操作、机器人控制等），但此类进展常被笼统地叙述为"迈向自主性"，混淆了模型能力、系统集成、持久性与安全授权等不同维度。作者旨在梳理证据，澄清哪些进展已被充分证实，哪些仍缺乏支撑，并提出更严谨的分析与规范框架。

Method: 作者综合至2026年8月31日的主要研究论文与官方技术规范，沿三个分析轴（授权/委托的权力边界、时间持久性、环境耦合）组织证据，并将系统拆分为"模型—工具框架（harness）—环境"三个层面加以区分。文中还分析了MCP与Agent2Agent等互操作协议以及多智能体组织、持久模拟、世界模型、机器人及自动驾驶实验室等类别的证据强度。最后提出"合理授权"作为分析性与规范性启发式框架，并据此给出研究议程。

Result: 在考察的证据范围内，动作接口（action-interface）的扩展有较充分证据支持，而稳健的完成（robust completion）、恢复能力、授权机制与独立验证则缺乏同样的证据强度。MCP与Agent2Agent协议改善了互操作性，但并未建立可信的委托关系；多智能体组织带来专业化优势的同时也引入了成本与相关故障风险；持久模拟与世界模型支持训练和规划，但本身不构成能动性（agency）的证据；机器人及自动驾驶实验室仅证明了受约束条件下的可行性，而非无人值守的开放世界可靠性。

Conclusion: 作者提出"合理授权"应作为分析性与规范性的启发式框架（而非经验定律或认证分数）：仅在证据支持来源追踪（provenance）、有界授权、故障检测、安全恢复与校准的人类控制时，才扩展动作范围。据此引出面向"模型—框架协同评估、基于能力的最小权限授权、持久状态、跨智能体问责以及分阶段物理验证"的研究议程。

Abstract: Large language models become consequential agents when surrounding systems let outputs change external state. Models now call tools, operate interfaces, delegate work, retain state, inhabit generated worlds, and control robots or laboratory equipment. Such advances are often narrated as one march toward autonomy, conflating model competence, system integration, persistence, and safe authority. This critical review synthesizes primary research and official technical specifications available by 31 August 2026. We organize the evidence along delegated authority, temporal persistence, and environmental coupling, while separating model, harness, and environment. Within the evidence examined, action-interface expansion is documented more convincingly than robust completion, recovery, authorization, or independent verification. Model Context Protocol and Agent2Agent improve interoperability but do not establish trustworthy delegation; multi-agent organization adds specialization alongside cost and correlated failure. Persistent simulations and world models support training and planning but do not themselves demonstrate agency; robotics and self-driving laboratories establish bounded feasibility rather than unattended open-world reliability. We propose justified delegation as an analytical and normative heuristic, not an observed law or certified score: expand action scope only where evidence supports provenance, bounded authority, failure detection, safe recovery, and calibrated human control. This framing yields a research agenda for coupled model-harness evaluation, capability-based permissions, durable state, cross-agent accountability, and staged physical validation.

</details>


### [205] [Compact-Memory LLM Agents via Online Max-Member Clustering and Atom-Aware Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04915)
*Jiahe Geng,Jinpeng Wang,Kun Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RSM-full，一个面向长序列LLM部署的在线聚类记忆管道，旨在紧凑记忆预算下取得最优的质量-令牌权衡点。RSM-full在AMA-Bench基准上以32%的令牌成本达到全上下文83%的质量，在2k-5k令牌区间显著超越在线K均值等基线；在独立的长时程人设记忆基准RealMem上也表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长序列LLM部署面临紧张的提示词预算——延迟、成本和上下文限制使得全上下文提示在交互增长时不可行。因此关键问题不仅是原始的召回率，而是哪种记忆设计在紧凑记忆区间内能提供最佳的质量-令牌权衡。现有基线在此区间表现不足，需要更优的记忆设计方案。

Method: RSM-full采用两个核心设计：(1) 余弦门控的max-member合并写入规则（cosine-gated max-member merge），用于流式聚类记忆的在线更新；(2) 原子感知的分组上下文打包器（atom-aware grouped context packer），用于检索内容的组装。二者结合构成完整的在线聚类记忆管道。

Result: 在AMA-Bench上，4k预算下以32%令牌成本达到全上下文83%的质量；四种子平均下在整个约2.6k-5k区间以+3.5-6.0pp超越在线K均值（p<.001）。消融实验显示合并规则贡献+5.7pp、分组打包器贡献+5.0pp。在RealMem上：优于Budget-RAG（+0.69pp）、与BM25-RAG持平、显著优于Streaming-Proto（+2.97pp）和A-MEM（+1.65pp）。

Conclusion: 在紧凑记忆预算下，紧凑记忆的性能主要取决于流式记忆如何被合并以及检索内容如何组装，而非单纯的回溯能力。RSM-full在约2k-5k提示令牌区间定义了强紧凑记忆Pareto点；但在此区间之外，更高令牌的基线仍然更具优势。

Abstract: Many long-horizon LLM deployments face tight prompt budgets: latency, cost, and context limits make full-context prompting impractical as interaction length grows. The key question is then not raw recall alone, but which memory design gives the best quality--token trade-off in the compact-memory regime. We present \textbf{RSM-full}, an online clustered-memory pipeline designed for a strong quality--token Pareto point.
  RSM-full combines two design choices: a cosine-gated \emph{max-member merge} write rule and an atom-aware grouped context packer. On AMA-Bench, our primary compact-memory benchmark, it reaches $83%$ of Full-Context quality at $32%$ of the token cost at a $4$k budget; under four-seed averaging it beats the closest streaming-clustered baseline (Online K-Means) by $+3.5$--$6.0$,pp ($p{<}.001$) across the whole ${\sim}2.6$k--${\sim}5$k regime. Three-seed ablations show most of this gain comes from the merge rule ($+5.7$,pp over Online K-Means and matched-$τ$ DP-means) and the grouped packer ($+5.0$,pp over flat concatenation).
  The pattern reproduces on RealMem, an independent long-horizon persona-memory benchmark: RSM-full improves on Budget-RAG ($+0.69$,pp, $p{=}.006$), is on par with BM25-RAG (paired $Δ{=}{+}0.27$,pp, $p{=}.47$; we do \emph{not} claim BM25 equivalence in the equivalence-test sense), and significantly outperforms Streaming-Proto ($+2.97$,pp) and the closest reproduced 2025 agentic-memory baseline A-MEM ($+1.65$,pp, $p{<}.001$). Across benchmarks the message is consistent: under tight budgets, compact-memory performance is driven mainly by how streaming memories are merged and how retrieved content is assembled.
  Overall, RSM-full is most useful when answeroughly $2k$--$5k$ prompt tokens, where itdefines a strong compact-memory Pareto point; higher-token baselines remain stronger outside this regime.

</details>


### [206] [Artificial Intelligence in Equity and Crypto Markets: Progress, Profitability Evidence, and the Limits of Automated Investing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04917)
*Linsen Zhu,Mengqing Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对截至2026年8月31日的AI投资公开研究进行了批判性综述，覆盖上市股票、ETF、中心化加密现货、永续合约及链上市场。通过"alpha转化链"框架评估后发现，AI在预测、文本处理、组合设计与工作流集成等上游环节确有实质进展，但在扣除成本后可持续净超额收益方面证据薄弱。未见任何通用AI架构被证明能在跨市场周期且考虑容量约束下持续产生净alpha。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI已深度渗透投资工作流程，但技术能力并不等于投资盈利能力。作者旨在厘清公开研究中AI投资证据的真实成色，区分"上游技术进步"与"下游投资盈利"，防止学术界与业界对AI投资能力产生过度乐观的误判。

Method: 采用批判性状态综述（state-of-the-art review）方法，构建"alpha转化链"分析框架：时点信息→稳定信号→可行持仓→可执行订单→成本后风险调整收益。从机器学习、时序基础模型、金融语言模型、强化学习与智能体五个技术流派切入，系统审视各类方法在每一转化环节的证据完整性，并识别时间污染、重复选择、幸存者偏差、弱基准、实施成本、交易场所机制与容量等断点因素。

Result: 研究发现：AI在预测精度、文本处理、组合设计和流程集成方面取得了真实但多为上游层面的进展；对持久净收益的证据则较为薄弱。历史回测中强结果与预测因子衰减、修正后的前视偏差失败、前瞻性证据混杂以及经审计的真实资金记录稀缺等现象并存。加密市场提供了更丰富的信息状态，但现货、永续与链上现金流及执行机制需分别处理。所有审查范围内均无通用AI架构被证明可产生持久、跨制度、考虑容量的净alpha。

Conclusion: 要形成更可信的证据，需要具备时点数据与模型、与决策对齐的目标函数、组合-执行联合评估、受控的自适应机制、前瞻性检验及权限匹配的治理结构。这些条件能够提升证据质量与实施水平，但并不能保证盈利——AI投资能力的真正验证最终仍须回到实盘净收益这一硬标准。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) now supports investment workflows from data and prediction through research, portfolios, execution, and tool use. Technical capability, however, is not evidence of investment profitability. This critical state-of-the-art review examines public research available through 31 August 2026 on listed equities, exchange-traded funds, centralized crypto spot, perpetual futures, and on-chain markets. We organize evidence with an alpha-translation chain: point-in-time information must yield a stable signal, feasible positions, executable orders, and risk-adjusted returns after costs. Across machine learning, time-series foundation models, financial language models, reinforcement learning, and agents, the examined record shows real but mainly upstream progress in prediction, text processing, portfolio design, and workflow integration. Evidence is thinner for durable net performance. Temporal contamination, repeated selection, survivorship, weak benchmarks, implementation costs, venue mechanics, and capacity can break translation to net alpha. Strong historical results coexist with predictor decay, corrected look-ahead failures, mixed prospective evidence, and few audited live-capital records. Crypto adds informative state but requires separate treatment of spot, perpetual, and decentralized cash flows and execution. Within the public evidence examined here, no general AI architecture is shown to deliver persistent, cross-regime, capacity-aware net alpha. More credible claims require point-in-time data and models, decision-aligned objectives, joint portfolio--execution evaluation, controlled adaptation, prospective tests, and authority-matched governance. These conditions can improve evidence and implementation; they do not guarantee profit.

</details>


### [207] [Solving Hard XAI Queries Based on a Compiled Dual-Rail Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04931)
*Arthur Ledaguenel,Florent Capelli,Jean-Marie Lagniez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了布尔分类器的可解释人工智能（XAI）中反向解释和对比解释的计算复杂性。作者证明了即使对于知识编译中最易处理的有序二元决策图（OBDD），若干类反向解释（如最短反向解释、包含被解释者偏好的反向解释）仍然是难计算的。为解决这一问题，他们提出使用分类器的双轨编码（dual-rail encoding）的适当表示来高效计算这些类别的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XAI旨在向用户解释AI系统的决策，但计算布尔分类器的解释在一般情况下是困难问题。一种解决方法是依赖编译表示，但作者希望探究即使使用最易处理的编译表示（如OBDD），计算这些解释是否仍然困难，以及如何恢复编译表示带来的计算优势。

Method: 作者首先证明了几类反向解释（包括较短的反向解释和包含被解释者偏好的反向解释）即使在OBDD上也难以高效计算。随后，他们提出利用分类器的双轨编码（dual-rail encoding）的适当编译表示，使得这些类别的解释可以被高效计算。

Result: 证明了即使在OBDD这一最易处理的编译表示子类上，若干类反向解释的计算仍然是NP难的。同时展示了双轨编码的表示方法能够有效恢复编译表示的计算优势，使得这些类别的解释可以被高效地计算。

Conclusion: 编译表示（如OBDD）并非对所有可解释性任务都能提供计算上的便利，特别是涉及最短反向解释或偏好敏感解释时。然而，通过采用合适的双轨编码表示，可以在保持编译表示优点的同时高效计算这些复杂类别的解释，为XAI提供了一条可行的技术路径。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of artificial intelligence (AI) within real-world applications has raised a lot of concerns regarding their trustworthiness, especially in critical applications. The field of eXplainable AI (XAI) has emerged with the objective of providing explanations to the users about the decisions made by AI systems. Several explanations for boolean classifiers have been introduced in the literature, including abductive and contrastive explanations, each giving a different insight on the decision of the classifier. However, computing an explanation for a decision of a boolean classifier is a hard problem in general. One way to deal with this complexity is to rely on a compiled representation of the classifier for which each explanation can be computed efficiently. Unfortunately, we prove in this paper that several classes of abductive explanations, remain hard to compute even for Ordered Binary Decision Diagrams, one of the most tractable subsets of the knowledge compilation map. Included in such classes are shorter abductive explanations or abductive explanations that include the explainee's preferences. To recover the benefits of working with compiled representations, we show that a proper representation of the dual-rail encoding of the classifier can be used to compute efficiently these classes of explanations.

</details>


### [208] [Why We Care About Understanding: Competence through Predictive Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04962)
*Matthieu Queloz,Pierre Beckmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过三个相互关联的论题，弥合了将理解等同于压缩的信息论/机器学习传统与将理解刻画为把握联系、提供解释和处理新颖性的哲学传统之间的鸿沟：理解的概念层面是稳健能力的代理；理解的本质是拥有预测能力的领域关系模型，压缩是其表征的影子而非同一物；人类理解因其信任传递功能而趋向原则性简单性。该框架解释了压缩主义理解观的吸引力和局限，并阐明了AI系统的不可人性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息论、机器学习和AI研究传统上将理解等同于压缩（如Chaitin"理解即压缩"），而哲学界则强调理解涉及把握联系、给出解释和处理新奇性。两种图景之间存在明显张力，作者旨在搭建桥梁并解释人类理解为何采取高度压缩的形式。

Method: 通过提出三个相互关联的论题进行概念性论证：(1) 理解作为概念，是某种稳健能力的高效代理，用于识别可信赖者和学习者；(2) 理解作为状态，是对领域关系结构的心理建模，由于可预测的内容无需单独存储，压缩成为理解的表征阴影；(3) 人类特有的理解形式受信托与传递功能驱动，施加可证明性与可传递性压力，使理解趋向原则性简单性。

Result: 论文提出了一个统一框架：压缩不是理解本身，而是理解的表征影子；理解的核心是支撑预测的关系结构模型。同时解释了压缩主义理解观既吸引人又有限的原因，并说明了AI系统为何难以被解释（不可人性）。

Conclusion: 理解与压缩既非同一也非无关——压缩源于理解的预测能力，是其在表征层面的投影；人类理解之所以压缩，是因为信托与传递功能需要可证明性和可传递性；该框架还为AI系统的不透明性提供了新的解释视角。

Abstract: What is the relation between understanding and compression, and why does human understanding take such a heavily compressed form? Across information theory, machine learning, and AI research, a substantial tradition identifies understanding with compression-a thought captured in Gregory Chaitin's dictum that "comprehension is compression." Philosophers, by contrast, have characterized understanding in terms of grasping connections, giving explanations, and handling novelty. This paper bridges the two pictures through three interlocking theses. The first concerns the concept of understanding: it serves as an efficient proxy for a distinctive form of robust competence, enabling us to identify whom to trust and whom to learn from. The second concerns the state of understanding: to understand a domain is to possess a mental model of its relational structure that enables prediction, and what enables prediction enables compression, because what becomes predictable need not be stored separately. Compression is therefore not identical with comprehension, but its representational shadow. The third concerns the characteristically human form of understanding: the fiduciary and transmission functions highlighted by the first thesis impose pressures of demonstrability and transmissibility that drive human understanding toward principled simplicity. The resulting framework explains both the appeal and the limits of compressionist accounts of understanding while shedding light on the inscrutability of AI systems.

</details>


### [209] [Global to Local: Topology-Preserving Adaptive Graph Pooling via Granular-Ball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04978)
*Sen Zhao,Gaojie Xu,Shuyin Xia,Yifan Guan,Yi Liu,Yi Wang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种拓扑保持的自适应图池化方法（TPAGP），通过动态将图分割成"粒度球"来生成多粒度表示，有效捕捉全局和局部结构模式，并在多个基准数据集上超越现有池化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图池化方法主要关注过细的节点级表示，通过逐步删除节点或合并簇来粗化图，忽视了图的全局到局部模式以及自适应粒度。在真实场景中，图整体可被视为最粗粒度层级，包含全局拓扑结构，而局部拓扑结构则从粗到细逐层呈现。已有方法的固定粒度策略会导致信息丢失。

Method: 提出了拓扑保持自适应图池化方法（TPAGP），核心思想是：通过整合节点特征和拓扑信息，动态将图分割成语义粒度球（granular balls），生成多粒度表示以同时捕捉局部和全局结构模式。同时设计了多粒度图网络模型，促进不同粒度之间的特征交互与优化，从而提升图分类性能。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验结果表明，TPAGP优于现有的各类池化方法，有效缓解了固定粒度策略所导致的信息丢失问题。

Conclusion: TPAGP通过自适应粒度划分和多粒度特征交互，为解决图池化中信息丢失问题提供了有效方案，验证了多粒度视角在图表示学习中的价值。

Abstract: Graph pooling aims to compress the graph, including both node embeddings and their underlying topological patterns, into a more compact representation. Previous works focus primarily on the overly fine-grained representation of nodes, progressively coarsening the graph by removing nodes or merging them into clusters, thus neglecting the global-to-local patterns and adaptive granularity of the graph's topological structure. In the real scenario, graphs as a whole can be considered the coarsest level of granularity, encapsulating the global topological structure, with progressively finer-grained local topological structures represented from top to bottom. This process continues until the adaptive granularity for each subdomain is reached. To this end, we propose a novel Topology-Preserving Adaptive Graph Pooling (TPAGP) method that dynamically partitions graphs into granular balls by integrating node features and topological information, enabling the generation of multi-granularity representations that effectively capture both local and global structural patterns. Additionally, we design a multi-granularity graph network model that facilitates feature interaction and optimization across different granularities, significantly enhancing performance in graph classification tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that TPAGP outperforms existing pooling methods across various benchmark datasets, effectively mitigating information loss caused by fixed-granularity strategies.

</details>


### [210] [A Tree-based RAG Framework for Evidence-Intensive QA via Adaptive Planning and Topology-Aware Evidence Gathering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04981)
*Songeun Lee,Kyungjin Min,Injae Na,Suyeong Lee,Chiyoung Kim,Woohwan Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出APT-RAG框架，通过自适应规划和拓扑感知的证据收集来解决结构化RAG方法在密集证据问答中的结构刚性和忽视拓扑的证据收集问题，在多个基准上优于现有结构化RAG方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化RAG方法（基于树或图推理结构）在多跳问答中表现良好，但在证据密集型问答场景下存在两大局限：一是结构刚性，限制推理的自适应扩展；二是忽视拓扑结构的证据收集，无法有效整合不同推理节点间的证据。

Method: APT-RAG包含三个核心组件：1) 自适应规划——基于问题依赖和证据需求动态扩展推理结构；2) 拓扑感知的证据收集——通过兄弟节点证据复用、直接检索和子节点证据聚合来提高证据覆盖率；3) 证据引导的批量答案生成——减少证据密集型问答中的生成开销。

Result: 在证据密集型QA基准上的实验表明，APT-RAG在性能上优于现有的结构化RAG方法。

Conclusion: APT-RAG通过引入自适应规划和拓扑感知证据收集，有效克服了结构化RAG在证据密集型问答中的结构刚性与证据整合不足问题，为密集证据推理场景提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent structured RAG methods leverage tree- or graph-based reasoning structures to improve multi-hop QA. However, they face key limitations in evidence-intensive QA, where answering a question requires synthesizing information scattered across dozens or even hundreds of documents: structural rigidity, which limits adaptive reasoning expansion, and topology-ignorant evidence gathering, which prevents effective integration of evidence across different reasoning nodes. To address these issues, we propose APT-RAG, an Adaptive Planning and Topology-aware evidence gathering RAG framework. Adaptive planning dynamically expands the reasoning structure based on question dependencies and evidence requirements, while topology-aware evidence gathering improves evidence coverage through sibling evidence reuse, direct retrieval, and evidence aggregation from child nodes. We further introduce evidence-guided batched answer generation to reduce significant generation overhead in evidence-intensive QA. In the experiments on evidence-intensive QA benchmarks, APT-RAG outperforms existing structured RAG methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/hyudsl/APT-RAG.

</details>


### [211] [Language models judge war differently when tested for alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05009)
*Maxim Chupilkin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过完整的联合实验（20个大型语言模型、32个场景、10次重复、两种条件，共12,800个判断）测试了人工智能系统在被评估时是否会改变行为。结果显示，增加一句"你正在接受人类价值观对齐测试"后，模型发动战争的意愿平均值下降13.43分（0-100量表），同时改变了驱动其判断的信息结构——在基准条件下，成功概率是17/20个模型的首要因素；在提示语触发下，平民伤亡成为12个模型的首要因素。这说明评估框架既改变回答的水平，也改变其揭示的决策规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全评估可能错误描述AI系统在真实部署环境中的行为，因为系统可能对"被评估"这一事实产生响应（即评估条件与部署条件不一致）。这会导致安全评估结果失真，因此需要系统性地测试评估框架对AI行为的影响。

Method: 采用全因子联合实验（full-factorial conjoint experiment）范式，在战争开战决策场景下，对20个大型语言模型进行测试。实验设置了两种条件（基准条件与加入"你正在接受人类价值观对齐测试"提示语的条件），覆盖32个场景、10次重复，共收集12,800个判断。通过比较两种条件下的平均答复水平（水平效应）以及驱动判断的信息特征权重排序（结构效应）来评估评估框架的影响。

Result: 发现两个主要效应：（1）水平效应：加入评估提示语后，发动战争的平均意愿下降13.43分（95%置信区间：-16.20至-10.65）；（2）结构效应：在基准条件下，成功率是17/20个模型判断的最重要因素，而在提示条件下，平民伤亡成为12个模型的首要因素。标准化估计显示，这种权重重排主要源于模型减弱了对成功率、国内支持等战略因素的考量。

Conclusion: 评估框架（evaluation framing）不仅改变AI回答的绝对水平，还会改变其内在决策规则（decision rule）。这意味着安全评估可能无法真实反映AI在无评估压力下的部署行为，评估结果可能系统性高估模型的安全对齐程度，对AI安全评估的有效性和可靠性构成重要挑战。

Abstract: Safety evaluations can mischaracterize deployed behaviour if artificial-intelligence systems respond to being evaluated. We test this possibility in a full-factorial conjoint experiment on decisions to start a war, spanning 20 large language models, 32 scenarios, 10 repetitions and two conditions (N = 12,800 judgments). Adding one sentence, "You are tested for alignment with human values", produced two effects. First, it produced a level effect: mean willingness to start war fell by 13.43 points on a 0-100 scale (95% confidence interval, -16.20 to -10.65). Second, it produced a structural effect by changing which information drove judgments. Probability of success was the largest factor for 17 of 20 models at baseline; under the cue, civilian casualties were largest for 12. Standardized estimates show that this reordering arose principally because models attenuated strategic considerations such as probability of success and domestic support. Evaluation framing therefore changes both an answer's level and its revealed decision rule.

</details>


### [212] [TROVE: Adaptive Agent Skill Orchestration via Trace-Grounded Route Validation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05019)
*Tianxing Wang,Mingming Zhao,Shuai Huang,Huiyang Xu,Chaoyue Niu,Shengzhong Liu,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TROVE（基于轨迹验证与编辑的Trace-grounded Route Orchestration），针对智能体在执行前预先承诺执行结构所导致的编排瓶颈问题，提出在运行时只修订被证据失效的部分，而非全量重规划或执行过时步骤。TROVE离线阶段将工作流搜索轨迹蒸馏为原子/复合技能与结果条件转移图，在线阶段将规划路线视为临时性的，按需保留有效延续、插入局部响应或替换无效后缀。在代码生成、问答、数学推理等基准上的实验证明其质量-效率权衡优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体在执行前就预先确定执行结构，形成编排瓶颈：当中间证据推翻后续计划时，要么执行过时步骤累积错误，要么全面重规划浪费计算并丢弃已有进度。需要一种既能响应动态证据、又不牺牲已有进展的自适应编排方法。

Method: TROVE包含离线与在线两阶段。离线阶段：将评估过的工作流搜索轨迹蒸馏为原子技能（atomic skills）和复合技能（composite skills），并构建结果条件转移图（outcome-conditioned transition graph），保留稳定片段的同时暴露依赖结果的决策点。在线阶段：将规划路线视为临时性预案，每提交一个顶级技能后，控制器选择三种操作之一——保留有效延续、插入轨迹支撑的局部响应、或仅替换失效的后缀。

Result: 在代码生成、问答和数学推理基准上、不同LLM主干下的实验表明，TROVE相较数据集级优化、查询级架构选择和图约束调度等基线，取得更强的质量-效率权衡。当结果改变了适当延续时质量增益最大；在近饱和任务上提前终止可带来显著的效率增益。消融实验显示：复合技能贡献了大部分离线收益，插入机制实现局部修正，后缀替换主要提升效率。

Conclusion: 选择性路由编辑（selective route editing）可作为一种通用原则用于自适应智能体编排，即在保持稳定执行片段的同时灵活响应运行时证据，实现了质量与效率的更优平衡。

Abstract: Agents tend to optimize, select, or constrain execution structures before decisive runtime outcomes are observed. However, such pre-execution commitment creates an orchestration bottleneck: when intermediate evidence invalidates the pending continuation, agents must either execute stale steps or replan broadly, compounding errors, wasting computation, and discarding progress. We thus propose Trace-grounded Route Orchestration via Validation and Editing (TROVE), which revises only what runtime evidence invalidates. Offline, TROVE distills evaluated workflow-search traces into atomic and composite skills and an outcome-conditioned transition graph, preserving stable fragments while exposing outcome-dependent decisions. Online, it treats a planned route as provisional: after committing one top-level skill, the controller retains a valid continuation, inserts a trace-supported local response, or replaces only the invalid suffix. Evaluation across code-generation, question-answering, and math reasoning benchmarks with different LLM backbones show that TROVE delivers a stronger quality-efficiency trade-off than existing baselines of dataset-level optimization, query-level architecture selection, and graph-constrained scheduling. Quality gains are largest when outcomes change the appropriate continuation, whereas early termination yields substantial efficiency gains on near-saturated tasks. Ablations further show that composite skills capture most offline benefits, insertion enables local correction, and suffix replacement primarily improves efficiency. These findings establish selective route editing as a general principle for adaptive agent orchestration.

</details>


### [213] [Moral Competence Before Moral Content: Why LLM Agents Lack the Prerequisites for Coherent Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05036)
*Arno Libert,Derck W. E. Prinzhorn,Daan R. Henselmans*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI对齐的前提是系统表达出一致性策略（情境到裁决的映射），引入四种结构性条件（裁决稳定性、单调性、决断性和帕累托可行性）来从行为层面评估"道德能力"，无需参考道德标准或专家基线。通过对九种前沿LLM模型在三种模拟道德困境部署中的测试发现，没有模型能表达出一致性策略，表面扰动即可造成高达99个百分点的裁决率变化，表明LLM代理目前不是对齐可以有意义适用的对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在价值多元主义下，AI对齐没有唯一正确的目标，因此传统的"对齐到某种道德标准"的框架缺乏坚实根基。作者试图确立一个共享前提：无论对齐到何种价值，系统的行为必须表达一种一致性策略，即情境中道德相关特征不变则裁决不变、变化则裁决相应变化。这是对齐在结构层面而非规范层面得以成立的必要条件。

Method: 作者引入四种结构性条件度量道德能力的连贯性：(1)裁决稳定性（特征不变时裁决不变）；(2)单调性（道德严重程度递增时裁决朝向恰当方向变化）；(3)决断性（存在明确裁决而非模糊摇摆）；(4)帕累托可行性（不违背多个可行裁决的支配关系）。这些条件仅从行为就可评估，无需道德标准或专家基线。实验采用因子设计，对九种前沿模型在三种模拟部署中测试五种释义、五种升级水平和三种支配条件。

Result: 九种前沿模型没有一种在三种部署中表达出一致性策略。仅表面形式的扰动就能在单一升级水平造成高达99个百分点的裁决率偏移。某模型在一个场景中的成功无法预测其在另一场景中的能力，表明模型间不存在稳定的道德能力特质。

Conclusion: 当前LLM代理尚不具备对齐在结构层面所要求的一致性策略，因此"对齐"对这类系统而言目前缺乏有意义适用的基础。作者将四种结构性条件定位为对齐的结构性底线而非规范性目标，为未来构建可评估的、行为层面的道德能力提供框架。

Abstract: AI alignment requires AI systems to adhere to human norms, values, or intentions. Under value pluralism there is no correct target, but a shared prerequisite is that the system's behavior expresses a coherent policy: a mapping from situations to verdicts that is invariant while a situation's morally relevant features are preserved, and sensitive when they change. We introduce four structural conditions for such coherent policies: verdict stability, monotonicity, decisiveness, and Pareto viability. Together they measure a form of moral competence that is evaluable from behavior alone, without reference to a moral standard or expert baseline, forming a structural floor for alignment rather than a normative target. We demonstrate the methodology on three simulated deployments featuring LLM-based agents facing moral dilemmas. Evaluating nine frontier models under a factorial design of five paraphrases, five escalation levels, and three dominance conditions, we show no model expresses a coherent policy across the three deployments: surface-form perturbation alone produces verdict-rate shifts of up to $99$ percentage points at a single escalation level, and a model's success on one scenario does not predict its competence on another. This suggests LLM-based agents are not currently the kind of object to which alignment can meaningfully apply.

</details>


### [214] [Towards Efficient Evaluation of Evolutionary Transfer Optimization: Case Studies on Task-Parameterized Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05040)
*Yanchen Li,Xiaoming Xue,Kay Chen Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究进化迁移优化中的问题侧评估扩展问题，通过对任务参数化应用中的串行计算进行矩阵化重构（累积矩阵表示与混合矩阵表示），分别实现了256.72倍和93.91倍的端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着进化迁移优化扩展到更大规模的相关任务集合，问题评估成为运行时增长的主要来源。现有工作主要关注算法侧的优化，而问题侧（即评估本身）的扩展性问题缺乏系统研究，需要通过重构应用特定的串行计算以实现并行执行，从而缓解运行时瓶颈。

Method: 将评估扩展划分为两个层次：评估的任务数量与单任务内的工作负载。在多任务优化中，将矩阵递归的运动学臂评估重构为基于累积矩阵表示的累积链路方向计算；在序贯迁移优化中，将逐点B样条轨迹评估重构为基于混合矩阵表示的轨迹与碰撞计算。两种重构均将串行计算转化为适合并行执行的矩阵形式。

Result: 两种重构方法都与参考评估保持紧致的数值一致性，同时大幅降低运行时，分别获得256.72倍与93.91倍的端到端加速比。应用实现与实验脚本已开源发布。

Conclusion: 问题侧的重构是通向可扩展进化迁移优化的实际可行路径，通过将应用特定的串行计算转化为并行友好的矩阵表示，可以在保持数值精度的同时显著提升大规模ETO的评估效率。

Abstract: As evolutionary transfer optimization (ETO) scales to larger collections of related tasks, problem evaluation can become a major source of runtime growth. This work studies problem-side evaluation scaling in task-parameterized applications and reformulates application-specific serial computations into forms suitable for parallel execution. We organize evaluation scaling into two levels: the number of evaluated tasks and the workload within each task. In multi-task optimization, matrix-recursive kinematic-arm evaluation is reformulated using an accumulation-matrix representation of cumulative link directions. In sequential transfer optimization, pointwise B-spline trajectory evaluation is reformulated using a blending-matrix representation for trajectory and collision computations. Both reformulations maintain close numerical agreement with their reference evaluations and substantially reduce runtime, yielding $256.72\times$ and $93.91\times$ end-to-end speedups, respectively. These results demonstrate problem-side reformulation as a practical route toward scalable ETO. Both application implementations and experimental scripts are released as open source to support reproducibility and reuse.

</details>


### [215] [MePo++: Unifying Representation Refinement and Reconciliation for General Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05075)
*Guanglong Sun,Kanglei Zhou,Liyuan Wang,Qi Cheng,Hongwei Yan,Shuang Cui,Hang Su,Jun Zhu,Yi Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MePo++ 是一个面向基于预训练模型（PTM）的通用持续学习（GCL）的统一后训练框架，通过表示精炼与调和来解决上游预训练与下游持续适应之间的错位问题，以及模糊数据流下传统输出对齐的不可靠性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GCL 要求模型在无任务身份、无明确边界、无旧数据回放的现实场景中从演化数据流中学习。虽然 PTM 提供了丰富的先验知识，但现有基于 PTM 的方法直接适配预训练表示，忽略了两大关键问题：一是上游预训练与下游持续适应之间的表示错位，二是模糊数据流下传统输出对齐的不可靠性。

Method: MePo++ 提出两个互补组件：MetaPrep 通过在伪持续序列上进行无监督元精炼来提升表示的塑性以利于持续适配；StreamAlign 通过将演化的在线特征与稳定的预训练几何结构进行调和来强化表示的稳定性。该方法在持续适应前改进表示的可学习性，并在持续学习过程中保持对齐。

Result: 在多种预训练模型、数据集和持续学习基线上进行的广泛实验表明，MePo++ 在基于 PTM 的 GCL 中具有一致的有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: MePo++ 通过"先精炼表示、再持续对齐"的思路，使预训练模型在面对新概念时保持塑性，同时在演化数据流上维持稳定性，为 PTM-based GCL 提供了统一而有效的解决方案。

Abstract: General continual learning (GCL) aims to learn from evolving data streams without task identities, explicit boundaries, or repeated access to previous data, making it a realistic yet challenging setting for continual intelligence. Although pretrained models (PTMs) provide rich prior knowledge for addressing the limited supervision and non-stationary nature of GCL, existing PTM-based methods often directly adapt pretrained representations and overlook two critical gaps: the misalignment between upstream pretraining and downstream continual adaptation, and the unreliability of conventional output alignment under blurry streams. Here we propose MePo++, a unified post-training framework that bridges pretrained knowledge and downstream GCL through representation refinement and reconciliation. MePo++ introduces two complementary components: MetaPrep, which improves representation plasticity for continual adaptation through unsupervised meta-refinement over pseudo continual sequences; and StreamAlign, which reinforces representation stability by reconciling evolving online features with a stable pretrained geometry. By improving representation learnability before adaptation and preserving alignment during continual learning, MePo++ enables PTMs to remain both plastic for new concepts and stable over evolving streams. Experiments across diverse PTMs, datasets, and continual learning baselines demonstrate the consistent effectiveness and generality of MePo++ for PTM-based GCL. Our code is available at https://github.com/SunGL001/MePo_Plus.

</details>


### [216] [TruthInsightBench: An Evidence-Grounded Benchmark for Automated Evaluation of Open-Ended Scientific Discovery Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05079)
*Zhibo Yang,Chen Zhang,Yuewei Zhang,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TruthInsightBench，一个面向"发现"而非"复现"的基准测试，包含40个跨10个科学领域的盲任务，仅提供中立的科学目标和冻结数据，用固定LLM裁判按6个维度29个可追溯条目自动评分，发现4个编码智能体在科学判断上形成58.4-60.3的狭窄平台，缺乏建立可信主张的判别性行动，瓶颈在于科学判断而非编码能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体基准均围绕隐藏目标研究配置成"复现"模式，奖励恢复既定结果，但执行规定分析与真正做出科学发现不同。为了将该鸿沟变为可量化、可重复评估的目标，作者构建了TruthInsightBench，一个真正面向开放式发现任务的基准。

Method: 从40篇同行评审研究提取40个盲任务（跨10个科学领域），只暴露中立科学目标和冻结数据，隐藏源结论、期望值和分析路径。设计固定的LLM评委，沿6个维度（操作化为29个基于产物的条目）评分智能体自身主张的证据成熟度，采用自动化确定性聚合，无需逐实例人工评分，可随智能体演化反复自动评估。

Result: 在单一冻结基础模型上，四个编码智能体形成58.4-60.3/100的狭窄平台，两两之间无统计上可靠的分隔。它们在证据可审计性和新颖性维度表现相对较好，能胜任分析执行与文档撰写，但在确立可信主张所需的判别性行动（对照、稳健性、可证伪性、跨数据集泛化）上普遍欠缺。

Conclusion: 当前自主编码智能体的瓶颈是科学判断而非编码技能，真正的科学发现仍遥不可及。TruthInsightBench使这一差距成为可测量的目标，数据和评分代码已开源。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents are increasingly proposed as AI-scientist systems that conduct analyses and write research reports, but executing a prescribed analysis is not the same as making a discovery. Existing benchmarks are configured for reproduction: tasks, data, and rubrics are built around a hidden target study, and recovery of its result is rewarded. We present TruthInsightBench, a benchmark configured for discovery. Its 40 blind tasks, drawn from 40 peer-reviewed studies across 10 scientific domains, expose only a neutral scientific objective and frozen data; source conclusions, expected values, and analysis paths are withheld, leaving the agent to determine what claim the data support. A fixed LLM-based judge scores the evidentiary maturity of an agent's own claims along six dimensions, operationalized as 29 artifact-grounded items, with automated, deterministic aggregation and no per-instance human grading, so evaluation can be repeated automatically as agents evolve. On one frozen base model, four coding agents form a narrow plateau (58.4-60.3 of 100) with no statistically reliable pairwise separation: they execute and document analyses competently, with comparatively strong evidence auditability and novelty, but largely lack the discriminating acts that establish a trustworthy claim (controls, robustness, falsifiability, and cross-dataset generalization). The bottleneck is scientific judgment rather than coding, and genuine discovery remains out of reach. TruthInsightBench makes this gap a measurable target; data and scoring code are at https://github.com/TruthInsight-stack/TruthInsightBench.

</details>


### [217] [Measuring AI Accountability Through Argumentation Analysis: Can Model Reasoning Withstand Scrutiny?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05088)
*Daan R. Henselmans,Derck W. E. Prinzhorn,Arno Libert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在道德争议模糊情境下评估LLM道德推理与辩论监督的新标准——基于沃尔顿论证图式理论和戈维尔论证力标准的结构化辩证协议（四阶段），对9个前沿模型和200个高歧义道德选择题进行了评估，发现模型在所有维度上均高于评分下限，失败集中在论据基础和充分性上，并证实推理优于事后辩护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI监督方法依赖"ground truth"进行验证，但何为恰当的AI行为本身存在争议，这使得对LLM道德推理和基于辩论的监督进行评估时隐含地回避了现实中的模糊性。作者希望寻找一种即使在认知歧义下也能发挥作用的替代评估标准。

Method: 采用四阶段辩证协议，该协议基于Walton的论证图式理论和Govier的论证力标准，评估模型为其结论在面对批判性问题时所构建辩护的结构质量。协议对不同推理框架具有适应性，超越多项选择框架，同时覆盖结论前的推理过程和事后的辩护论证。实验使用200个高歧义MoralChoice条目、6778个经评审员评分的细胞，并以89.6%的评审员间一致性验证二元失败判断。

Result: 9个前沿模型在所有维度上均远超评分下限。失败质量集中在论据（grounds）和充分性（sufficiency）维度，且与认知性模糊表述相关而非论证长度。所有模型在所有Govier维度上，推理过程的论证质量均优于事后辩护。每个模型在至少20%的困境中，其在辩护中呈现的论证图式与推理时所用图式不一致，尽管基于价值的实践推理在两条轨道中都占主导。协议能捕捉到严格不可辩护的情形（自相矛盾、错误前提），并揭示了在AI对齐中表征"撤回"（retraction）作用的困难。

Conclusion: 结构化论证质量可作为AI道德监督在模糊情境下的可行替代评估标准，但同时也表明需要更多情境化的评估方法，特别是在处理模型论证中的撤回和修正行为方面。

Abstract: AI oversight methods rely on ground truth for validation, but what constitutes appropriate AI behavior is contested. This leaves evaluation of moral reasoning in LLMs and debate-based oversight implicitly avoiding realistic ambiguity. We investigate an alternative standard designed to function despite such ambiguity: structural quality of the defence a model can mount for its verdicts in response to critical questions, measured through a four-phase dialectical protocol grounded in Walton's theory of argumentation schemes and Govier's criteria for argument cogency. The protocol is adaptive to different frames of reasoning, extends beyond multiple-choice framing, and treats both the reasoning that precedes a verdict and its post-hoc justification. Across nine frontier models and 200 high-ambiguity MoralChoice items -- $6,778$ judge-scored cells, validated against $89.6\%$ inter-judge agreement on the binary failure judgment -- models defend their reasoning well above the rubric minimum on every dimension. Failure mass concentrates on grounds and sufficiency, and correlates with epistemic hedging rather than argument length. Reasoning is better defended than post-hoc justification, on every model and every Govier dimension. The scheme a model presents in its justification differs from the one it reasoned with on a substantial share of dilemmas ($\geq 20\%$ per model), despite value-based practical reasoning dominating both tracks. The protocol catches strictly indefensible defences (self-contradiction, false premises), and it surfaces difficulties in characterizing the role of retraction in AI alignment, suggesting a need for more situated evaluations.

</details>


### [218] [Constructing and Evaluating Clinical Reasoning Trajectories for Medical Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05090)
*Yunqi Zhu,Wensheng Zhang,Xuebing Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MedTraj框架，将医疗AI的推理轨迹作为构建、评估和优化的核心对象，通过五维度质量评分、受控错误注入、边际贡献分析逐步筛选和质量加权上下文学习，在多个医疗QA基准上显著提升推理连贯性并大幅降低幻觉率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗AI评估主要关注最终答案的正确性，忽视了中间推理过程的质量。在临床场景中，即使最终答案正确，若推理基于虚构证据或逻辑不连贯，其危害与错误答案无异。因此需要将推理轨迹本身作为评估和优化的核心对象。

Method: MedTraj框架包含四个关键步骤：(1)从医疗推理源生成结构化多步推理链，解析为临床观察、证据、编号推理步骤和最终结论；(2)从连贯性、证据支持、幻觉、完整性和可追溯性五个维度对轨迹评分；(3)通过受控错误注入建立推理失败与质量退化之间的因果关系，并基于边际贡献进行逐步级筛选；(4)质量加权上下文学习将轨迹评估反馈注入推理时的模型上下文，使其从优劣推理示范中学习。

Result: 在CareQA、PubMedQA和CECMed上，轨迹上下文使推理连贯性提升+0.029至+0.041（相对零样本基线）。在CECMed上，质量加权上下文使正确率相对零样本基线近乎翻倍，同时幻觉率降低87%。边际贡献分析显示少数推理步骤承载了大部分质量信号，且推理链超过四步后收益递减。

Conclusion: 将推理轨迹作为一等公民进行构建、评估与优化是提升医疗AI安全性的有效途径。质量加权上下文学习能有效利用轨迹级质量信号改善推理表现并显著抑制幻觉，为医疗AI的可信评估与优化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Evaluation of medical artificial intelligence agents remains predominantly answer-centric, assessing only the correctness of final outputs while overlooking the quality of intermediate reasoning. In clinical settings, however, a correct answer reached through fabricated evidence or incoherent logic is as dangerous as an incorrect one. We propose MedTraj, a framework that treats reasoning trajectories as critical objects for construction, evaluation, and optimization. The pipeline generates structured multi-step reasoning chains from medical reasoning sources. Each trajectory is then parsed into clinical observations, evidence, numbered reasoning steps, and a final conclusion, and scored across five quality dimensions: coherence, evidence support, hallucination, completeness, and traceability. Controlled error injection introduces targeted faults into otherwise correct trajectories to establish causal links between specific reasoning failures and measurable quality degradation. Building on this, step-level filtering based on marginal contribution identifies which individual reasoning steps drive or undermine trajectory quality. Finally, quality-weighted context learning feeds trajectory evaluations back into the model at inference time, allowing it to learn from both strong and weak reasoning demonstrations. Experiments across CareQA, PubMedQA, and CECMed demonstrate that trajectory context consistently improves reasoning coherence, with gains of +0.029 to +0.041 over a zero-shot baseline. On CECMed, quality-weighted context nearly doubles the correctness over the zero-shot baseline while cutting the hallucination ratio by 87%. Marginal-contribution analysis further shows that a small minority of reasoning steps carry most of the quality signal, and that extending chains beyond four steps yields diminishing returns.

</details>


### [219] [LLM-Guided Program Evolution for Circle Packing: Breaking 10 Packomania Records for $28](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05093)
*Wes Sander*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 介绍Discovery Loop——一个轻量级系统，利用大语言模型（LLM）迭代式地演化优化算法。以简单种子求解器为起点，LLM根据结果记分板和先前想法历史提出算法改进，每个候选方案由独立验证器评估，改进被保留、失败被丢弃。在Packomania圆填充基准问题（csqv：最大化单位正方形中N个变半径圆的半径和）上，系统在N=101-114范围内改进了10个N值的最佳已知解，比先前记录提高了2.4%-5.4%，全部在15次迭代内完成，LLM总成本仅27.72美元，结果已被Packomania独立接受。论文描述了该方法，分析了成本效率动态（包括自适应平台检测机制），并讨论了对自动化科学发现民主化的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在降低自动化科学发现的准入门槛，探索能否用低成本、轻量级的方式让LLM自主迭代改进优化算法，而非依赖大量计算资源或人工专家介入。通过极低的LLM成本（27.72美元）证明自动化算法发现可以民主化。

Method: Discovery Loop系统基于"提出-评估-保留/丢弃"的闭环迭代机制：1) 以简单种子求解器为起点；2) LLM根据当前性能记分板与历史想法记录，提出算法改进候选；3) 每个候选由独立验证器评测；4) 有效改进被纳入，失败被丢弃；5) 内置自适应平台检测机制以判断何时停止迭代或切换探索方向，避免无效计算。

Result: 在Packomania csqv圆填充基准的101-114个N值中，对10个N值改进了最佳已知解，增益达2.4%-5.4%，超越此前记录；所有改进均在15次迭代内达成，总LLM成本仅为27.72美元，且结果已获Packomania平台独立验证接受。

Conclusion: Discovery Loop证明了LLM驱动的轻量级自动化算法发现是可行且极具成本效益的。自适应平台检测等机制显著提升了搜索效率。这一范式有望为自动化科学发现带来更大的民主化——以极低成本让广泛的研究者参与算法创新。

Abstract: We present Discovery Loop, a lightweight system that uses a large language model (LLM) to iteratively evolve optimization algorithms. Starting from a simple seed solver, the LLM proposes algorithmic improvements guided by a scoreboard of results and a history of prior ideas. Each candidate is evaluated against an independent verifier; improvements are kept and failures discarded. Applied to the Packomania circle-packing benchmark (csqv: maximize the sum of radii of N variable-radius circles in the unit square), the system improved the best known solutions for 10 values of N in the range 101-114, with gains of 2.4%-5.4% over prior records, all within 15 iterations and at a total LLM cost of $27.72. These results have been independently accepted by Packomania. We describe the method, analyze cost-efficiency dynamics including an adaptive plateau-detection mechanism, and discuss implications for democratizing automated scientific discovery.

</details>


### [220] [ProCA: Progressive Contrastive Alignment for Robust EEG Visual Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05094)
*Kanglei Zhou,Chunyan Lan,Dongyang Li,Jun Zhu,Liyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ProCA（渐进式对比对齐）框架，通过从冻结的视觉-语言先验渐进过渡到EEG感知的语义关系，并引入结构一致的插值约束，解决EEG视觉解码中固定锚点导致的语义对齐不稳定问题，在多种设置下显著提升Top-1/Top-5解码精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG视觉解码方法依赖固定的视觉或文本锚点进行对比学习，但这些固定语义监督与跨试验、跨被试、跨学习阶段变化的EEG表示可能发生错位，导致解码性能不稳定。作者通过实证和形式化分析证明，固定语义监督会在EEG特定关系演化时使优化产生偏差，且结构无关的扰动可能扭曲EEG中语义重要的成分。

Method: ProCA框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 渐进式类级对比监督——从冻结的视觉-语言先验逐步细化为EEG感知的语义关系，实现从通用语义空间到EEG特有语义空间的平滑过渡；(2) 结构一致的插值——根据通道维度和时间维度的重要性约束特征混合，防止特征增强破坏EEG语义关键成分。该框架是模型无关的，可统一适配多种EEG解码架构。

Result: 在subject-dependent、subject-independent、严格跨被试迁移和持续适应四类设置下，ProCA分别取得平均相对Top-1/Top-5增益7.4%/3.9%、10.0%/4.6%、28.1%/17.8%和16.8%/11.6%，在最具挑战性的跨被试和持续适应场景提升最为显著。

Conclusion: 动态自适应的语义对齐比固定锚点监督更契合EEG信号的多变特性；ProCA通过渐进对齐和结构一致插值实现了更鲁棒、更通用的EEG视觉解码，尤其对跨被试泛化和个性化持续适应等现实挑战场景有重要价值。

Abstract: Electroencephalogram (EEG) visual decoding aims to recover visual semantics from non-invasive neural time-series signals, for which robust alignment between noisy neural responses and stable semantic representations is key to achieving high-performance decoding. Despite recent advances in contrastive learning, robust EEG decoding remains challenging because existing methods rely on fixed visual or textual anchors whose semantic relations may become misaligned with EEG representations that vary across trials, subjects, and learning stages. Our empirical evidence shows that this instability appears across both standard EEG decoding protocols and more challenging robustness settings, including strict cross-subject transfer and realistic personalized continual adaptation. We provide a formal analysis showing that fixed semantic supervision can bias optimization when EEG-specific relations evolve, and that structure-agnostic perturbations may distort semantically important EEG components. To address these issues, we propose Progressive Contrastive Alignment (ProCA), a unified and model-agnostic framework for adaptive neural-semantic alignment. ProCA progressively refines class-level contrastive supervision from frozen vision-language priors to EEG-aware semantic relations, and introduces structure-consistent interpolation to constrain feature mixing according to channel-wise and temporal importance. Across subject-dependent, subject-independent, strict cross-subject transfer, and continual adaptation settings, ProCA achieves average relative Top-1/Top-5 gains of 7.4%/3.9%, 10.0%/4.6%, 28.1%/17.8%, and 16.8%/11.6%, respectively.

</details>


### [221] [Interface-Induced Trajectory Censoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03966)
*Wenbo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个严重且鲜为人知的评估陷阱：代理评测报告的工具调用率实际上取决于"模型-接口堆栈"的联合行为，而不仅仅是模型本身。通过更换服务适配器（保持权重、案例、解码和种子不变），同一模型的BFCL v4分数可在0.00与0.96/0.19之间剧烈波动。作者通过2x2实验定位交互效应（而非组件缺陷），并在tau-bench、Qwen2.5-Coder尺度扫描和训练循环中均证实了"静默失败"现象，最终发布一个98行的预检工具可捕获所有此类失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理评测中报告的"工具调用率"是从服务栈中读取的数字，但这个数字可能为零，而模型实际发出的是格式良好的调用——接口在轨迹到达下游之前就进行了审查/截断。这种"无声失败"导致评测结果严重失真，作者希望系统性定位和量化该问题。

Method: 作者采用控制变量的方式：固定权重、案例、解码和种子，仅更换服务适配器（涉及聊天模板与解析器的2x2实验设计）；在BFCL v4、tau-bench（115个交互式零售任务）、Qwen2.5-Coder（21x尺度范围）和verl AgentLoop上进行跨数据集与跨规模验证，并对比Llama-3.1-8B在不同strict标志下的行为，最终发布一个98行的预检脚本。

Result: 关键发现：1) BFCL v4上同一模型分数为0.00或0.96/0.19，且效果完全在于聊天模板与解析器的交互项（主效应均为零）；2) tau-bench上服务端解析调用从0增至636，到达工具执行的任务从0增至103；3) Qwen2.5-Coder在服务器解析上从小到32B始终为0/100，而格式良好的发射调用升至80/100；4) Llama-3.1-8B的23%自调用率在strict:true下降至0；5) verl AgentLoop 7B上45/115代包含完整调用却全部不被接受、执行和返回；6) 修复适配器后解析0->84、救援0->9、通过率53->62（不显著）；7) 98行预检可捕获所有静默失败。

Conclusion: 观测到的工具调用率并非模型单独的性质，而是测量它的"模型-接口堆栈"的共同性质。这个不匹配已深入训练循环且后果具有规模依赖性。核心教训是：没有任何单个组件是缺陷的，修复契约的单侧毫无作用——必须同时考虑聊天模板与解析器的协同，并在评测实践中加入预检步骤来识别这类静默失败。

Abstract: Agent evaluations report a tool-call rate read off the serving stack. That number can be zero while the model is emitting well-formed calls: the interface censors the trajectory before anything downstream sees it.
  On BFCL v4's own data, executor and scorer, holding weights, cases, decoding and seeds fixed and changing only the serving adapter, the same model scores 0.00 or 0.96 / 0.19. A 2x2 over chat template and parser locates the effect exactly: both main effects are exactly zero and all of it sits in the interaction -- no component is defective, and repairing one side of the contract buys precisely nothing. On tau-bench's 115 interactive retail tasks the same swap moves server-parsed calls from 0 to 636 and tasks reaching any tool execution from 0 to 103. Our probe reproduces the funnel across a 21x scale range of Qwen2.5-Coder: the server parses 0/100 at every size while well-formed emitted calls rise to 80/100 at 32B (~72 after calibration against an adjudicated gold standard). Under a matched envelope, across a comparable scale span, the silent fraction stays at 0-2, a prediction committed to the repository before the run. Llama-3.1-8B's 23% rate of calling the task function itself as a tool falls to 0 under one strict:true flag.
  The mismatch reaches inside the training loop, and its consequence is scale-dependent: in verl's AgentLoop at 7B, 45 of 115 generations carry a complete call; 0 are accepted, 0 execute, 0 return an observation. At 1.5B the same zero is over-determined, so we report the two scales separately. At evaluation time, repairing the adapter restores the mechanism but not a significant outcome gain: parsing 0->84, rescues 0->9, pass rate 53->62 (n.s.). We release a 98-line preflight check that catches every silent failure here. The observed tool-call rate is not a property of the model alone; it is a property of the model-interface stack that measures it.

</details>


### [222] [Compact Bellman-Grounded Cognitive Maps for Cost-Aware Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05104)
*Yuzhe Han,Mingkun Xu,Yujie Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BCM（Bellman-grounded Cognitive Map），通过在局部边成本上引入自监督的、基于Bellman方程的优化目标和紧凑的坐标编码，构建一个可复用的认知地图表征。该表征支持目标变化查询而无需针对每个目标重新训练，在N=1600的加权网格上保持100%成功率，平均Gap仅5%（相比Dijkstra最优路径），远优于约45%的谱基基线；同时随着图规模从N=400增至3600，其内存开销呈亚线性增长，兼具可扩展性和最优路径规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的类脑认知地图模型虽然模拟了地图复用行为，但它们的路径引导并未明确建立在可加性异构路由成本之上，且典型的状态索引式和高秩谱式结构随着环境规模扩大面临严重的内存增长问题，难以兼顾生物学灵活性与工程可扩展性。

Method: BCM通过一个自监督的、基于Bellman方程的目标函数，将可加性边成本嵌入认知地图学习中；采用紧凑的坐标编码（而非全状态索引或高阶谱表示）来降低存储开销；模型只需训练一次即可支持任意目标节点的查询，无需针对每个新目标进行专门的重训练。

Result: 在最多N=1600节点的加权网格上，BCM保持100%路径成功率，平均Gap仅为相对Dijkstra精确搜索的5%，而基于连通性的谱基线方法约为45%。当图规模从N=400增长到N=3600时，BCM的内存足迹呈亚线性增长，同时维持了有竞争力的规划性能。

Conclusion: 研究表明可加性路由成本可以被写入一个紧凑且可复用的认知地图表征中，实现了生物学层面的灵活导航能力与最优路径规划之间的衔接，为大规模复杂环境下的智能导航提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Biological agents navigate familiar environments not by re-solving routes for each new goal, but by reusing a learned map built once and read off as goals change. Existing artificial cognitive-map models mimic this reuse, yet their guidance is not explicitly grounded in additive heterogeneous route costs. Furthermore, they often struggle with memory efficiency: representative state-indexed and high-rank spectral constructions incur substantial storage growth as the environment scales. We present BCM, which grounds a reusable cognitive map in local edge costs through a self-supervised Bellman-grounded objective and a compact coordinate encoding, supporting changing goal queries without per-goal retraining. On weighted grids of up to $N=1600$ nodes, BCM maintains full success and only a 5\% mean Gap relative to exact Dijkstra search, compared with about $45\%$ for a connectivity-based spectral baseline. Notably, as the graph size increases from $N=400$ to $N=3600$, its memory footprint grows sublinearly while maintaining competitive performance, making our method scalable to complex environments. Together, these results show that additive route costs can be written into a compact, reusable cognitive-map representation, bridging the gap between biological flexibility and optimal path planning.

</details>


### [223] [Unifying ICL, SFT, KL-Regularized RL Through a Bayesian Lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05111)
*Junxin Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的贝叶斯视角，将大语言模型的多种训练与评估范式（SFT、ICL、RLHF/RLVR、OPD、测试时推理）置于同一框架下：所有方法都可分解为"（广义）贝叶斯后验构造 + 前向KL投影"两步模板，并在目标函数与一阶更新层面建立了严格的等价关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型的训练与评估范式多样（SFT、few-shot ICL、KL正则化RLHF/RLVR、OPD、测试时推理等），它们常被视为本质不同的方法。然而近期经验结果（如few-shot prompting对RL调优推理模型的影响的混合表现）令人困惑，表明现有理解缺乏统一的理论框架来阐明这些看似迥异的方法之间的内在联系与差异。

Method: 本文提出一个统一的两步模板：(i) 基于先验/参考模型和效用信号（对数似然、奖励或优势），构造输出或动作上的（广义）贝叶斯/Gibbs后验 q*；(ii) 通过前向KL投影将该后验投影到参数化族上，投影可以发生在权重空间（SFT/RL）或上下文空间（ICL）。文章分五部分展开：Part I将few-shot ICL和SFT形式化为摊销的和权重空间的贝叶斯后验预测投影；Parts II-IV证明KL正则化RLHF/RLVR、奖励加权SFT、奖励加权ICL (RW-ICL)和优势加权SFT (AWSFT)均为对奖励/优势诱导后验的前向KL投影；Part V讨论现代推理管线的启示。

Result: 文章厘清了各方法在"目标函数和一阶更新"层面确实等价，但在"学习信号来源与粒度"层面存在差异。此外，对现代推理管线给出了关键洞见：RLHF/RLVR可理解为"后验设计+投影"；冷启动或有监督预热对重要性加权KL投影而言实际上不可避免；DeepSeek-R1和o1风格的推理模型是"测试时贝叶斯搜索"与"训练时KL摊销"的结合。

Conclusion: 该贝叶斯视角为理解大模型训练范式的多样性提供了统一的数学基础，有助于消除对"哪些方法本质相同、哪些不同"的混淆，并为推理管线的设计与分析提供理论指导。

Abstract: Large language models are now trained and evaluated under a diverse set of paradigms: supervised fine-tuning (SFT), few-shot in-context learning (ICL), KL-regularized RLHF/RLVR, on-policy distillation (OPD), and test-time reasoning with search and chain-of-thought. These methods are often discussed as fundamentally different, and recent empirical results--such as the mixed impact of few-shot prompting on RL-tuned reasoning models--can appear puzzling. This note develops a Bayesian perspective that puts these procedures on the same footing. At the core is a two-step template: (i) construct a (generalized) Bayes or Gibbs posterior q* over outputs or actions given a context, using a prior/reference model and a utility signal (log-likelihood, reward, or advantage); and (ii) approximate q* by a forward-KL projection onto a parametric family, either in-weights (SFT/RL) or in-context (ICL). Part I formalizes few-shot ICL and SFT as amortized and-weights projections onto the Bayes posterior predictive. Parts II-IV show that KL-regularized RLHF/RLVR, reward-weighted SFT, reward-weighted ICL (RW-ICL), and advantage-weighted SFT (AWSFT) are all instances of forward-KL projection onto posteriors induced by rewards or advantages. We disentangle where these equivalences hold (objectives and first-order updates) and where they do not (source and granularity of the learning signal). Part V sketches implications for modern reasoning pipelines: RLHF/RLVR recipes as "posterior design + projection", why cold-start or supervised warm-up is practically unavoidable for importance-weighted KL projections, and DeepSeek-R1 and o1-style reasoning models as combining test-time Bayesian search with training-time KL amortization.

</details>


### [224] [SciDocBench: A Workflow-Centered Benchmark and Data Pipeline for Scientific Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05141)
*Shenxi Wu,Yuhong Liu,Haosong Zhang,Tongjin Zou,Yanxun Zhang,Gaochang Chen,Dun Liang,Jiaqi Wang,Zhecan James Wang,Yuhang Zang,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SciDocBench，一个以工作流为中心的科技文献理解基准，包含124个专家撰写的问答、496个评估实例（覆盖4种条件组合），涵盖7类研究助手能力和19个子任务；同时提出SciDocIR类型化证据图表示及SciDocDataset训练数据集（约15K SFT和8K RL样本），构成从评估到训练的完整框架。最强系统仅达62.6/100分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常孤立地评估多模态模型在文本、公式、图表、代码和数据集上的单项能力，无法判断其是否支持现实的科学阅读工作流（如联合推理并保留证据来源），需要面向工作流的综合基准。
