Across Search-R1-style retrieval and DeepEyes-style perception RL, applying PTA before downstream RL improves macro best@4 by 2.5 and 2.8 points over OPKD under the same downstream RL budget, while lookahead improves throughput by 24%.

</details>


### [291] [Dynamic Heterogeneous Graph Representation Learning: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04779)
*Huan Liu,Pengfei Jiao,Jie Yin,Hongjiang Chen,Zhidong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是首篇针对动态异质图（DHG）表示学习的系统性综述，从时间粒度视角统一了离散时间和连续时间DHG的形式定义，并提出了以算法为中心的创新分类体系（嵌入方法、GNN方法、Transformer方法），同时总结了典型应用、数据集和未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的AI系统通常表现为随时间演化的异质实体及其复杂交互，而传统的静态或同质图建模方法难以有效处理此类动态异质结构。已有的研究工作分散且缺乏统一视角，亟需对动态异质图表示学习方法进行系统梳理和总结。

Method: 作者首先从时间粒度角度提出了一个统一的形式化定义，涵盖离散时间和连续时间两类DHG；进而提出了一种新颖的以算法为中心的体系分类，将现有文献划分为早期基于嵌入的方法、基于图神经网络（GNN）的模型和较新的基于Transformer的DHG方法，并明确指出了这些方法在动态粒度上的内在建模偏置；此外还总结了DHG的典型应用场景及常用数据集与基准。

Result: 完成了对动态异质图表示学习领域的首次系统性审查，统一了形式定义，构建了算法导向的分类框架，识别了不同方法在动态粒度处理上的建模偏置，并整理了应用与基准资源。

Conclusion: 本文通过系统性综述为DHG表示学习领域提供了清晰的图谱，指明了具有前景的研究方向，为后续研究者在该快速发展的领域中开展工作提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Graph representation learning (GRL) serves as a canonical paradigm for modeling complex networks. However, real-world AI systems inherently manifest as evolving heterogeneous entities with complex interactions, posing significant challenges to static or homogeneous modeling. To address these complexities, representation learning for Dynamic Heterogeneous Graphs (DHGs) has emerged as a vital approach for learning low-dimensional representations that simultaneously preserve structural semantics and temporal dynamics. This survey presents the first systematic review of DHG representation learning methods. We first introduce a unified formal definition that encompasses both discrete-time and continuous-time DHGs from the perspective of temporal granularity. Building upon this formulation, we propose a novel algorithm-centric taxonomy that categorizes existing literature, including early embedding-based approaches, graph neural network (GNN)-based models, and relatively recent Transformer-based DHG methods, while explicitly highlighting their intrinsic modeling biases with respect to dynamic granularity. Furthermore, we summarize representative applications of DHG representation learning, along with commonly used datasets and benchmarks. Finally, we discuss promising research directions that guide future advances in this rapidly evolving field.

</details>


### [292] [Learning-Augmented Algorithms: Guarantees, Construction Mechanisms, and System-Level Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04787)
*Hailiang Zhao,Peng Chen,Xueyan Tang,Jianwei Yin,Shuiguang Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是对学习增强型算法（learning-augmented algorithms）的系统性综述，综合了预测接口、误差度量、一致性-鲁棒性权衡，以及在线优化、缓存、学习数据结构、图问题和机制设计等领域的五种代表性构造机制。文章区分了定理层面的上界结果与匹配的渐近依赖，将形式化保证与经验性系统证据分离，并明确处理了预测代价、反馈与组合问题，最终阐述了有限端到端推理的充分条件，并指出了代价感知预测、内生误差、语义预测器和基准测试等开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习增强算法利用可能出错的预测信息同时保持形式化性能保证，但该领域缺乏统一的综合视角。不同子领域（在线优化、缓存、数据结构等）对预测接口、误差质量和构造机制的定义各不相同，导致研究成果分散、可比性不足。作者旨在通过系统化综合，建立跨领域的统一分析框架，识别正交的理论轴与经验证据之间的界限，从而推动该领域的理论与实践整合。

Method: 采用综述性研究方法：首先综合预测接口（prediction interfaces）和误差度量（error measures），其次分析一致性-鲁棒性权衡（consistency-robustness trade-offs），再次归纳五种代表性构造机制，并将其应用于多个应用领域。方法论上还采用了定理级轴（区分上界与匹配渐近依赖）将理论结果分类，同时将形式化保证与经验系统证据明确分离，并考察预测代价、反馈信号和算法组合对端到端推理能力的影响。

Result: 得到了跨领域的综合框架：明确了在不同预测设置下构造学习增强算法的系统化路径，提出了有限端到端推理成立的充分条件，并识别出四个关键开放研究方向（代价感知预测、内生误差、语义预测器与基准测试）。这些结果表明当前理论保证与实证效果之间的鸿沟可以为后续研究提供明确纲领。

Conclusion: 该综述为学习增强算法提供了统一的分析视角，在理论深度与系统实践之间作出了明确区分。通过界定可推理范围的充分条件并列出待解决的开放问题，作者认为该领域正处于从孤立定理向整体设计理论的过渡阶段，未来需要同时关注预测成本的真实建模和跨层语义预测器的开发。

Abstract: Learning-augmented algorithms use fallible predictions while retaining formal performance guarantees. This survey synthesizes prediction interfaces, error measures, consistency--robustness trade-offs, and five representative construction mechanisms across online optimization, caching, learned data structures, graph problems, and mechanism design. An orthogonal theorem-level axis distinguishes achieved upper bounds from matched asymptotic dependence. Formal guarantees are separated from empirical systems evidence, with explicit treatment of prediction cost, feedback, and composition. The resulting synthesis states sufficient conditions for limited end-to-end reasoning and delineates open problems in cost-aware prediction, endogenous error, semantic predictors, and benchmarking.

</details>


### [293] [How Faithful Is Attribution for Sales Forecasting? A Counterfactual Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04797)
*Glib Kechyn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为多序列WaveNet销量预测模型增加了一个与架构无关的反事实可解释性层，可通过严格的删除/插入实验证明属性归因的真实性，并诚实地刻画了归因信息在哪些方面有效及存在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度销量预测模型（如WaveNet风格的膨胀卷积网络）虽然准确但缺乏可解释性，尤其是多序列模型中单一模型预测多条序列时无法解释预测原因。现有可加性SHAP式归因方法会产生分配伪影（allocation artifacts），因此需要一种能精确分解预测贡献且避免此类伪影的可解释方法。

Method: 在Corporacion Favorita全量杂货数据集（174,685条序列、1,688天）上训练多序列WaveNet预测器，添加一个post-hoc的、与架构无关的反事实可解释层。该方法将每个预测分解为多个贡献，且这些贡献之和精确等于预测值。采用删除/插入（deletion/insertion）协议评估归因的忠实性，并跨5个背景采样种子进行鲁棒性检验。

Result: 删除/插入协议均显示统计显著效应（删除差距0.22，p<0.001；插入差距0.27，p<0.01），跨5个种子鲁棒。促销信号的归因依赖在不同序列间异质性明显（中位数比率约1.0，约20%序列有强效应）。模型能捕捉周销售周期形状（周内日相关系数r=0.78）但系统性地低估其振幅。

Conclusion: 本文贡献不在于提升预测精度，而在于提供一个经过严格忠实性评估的可解释层，并诚实阐述其信息性与局限边界，为深度销量预测模型的可解释性和可信度分析提供方法论参考。

Abstract: Deep models for sales forecasting, such as WaveNet-style dilated convolutional networks, are accurate but opaque: when a single model predicts sales for one of many series, it offers no account of why. We add a post-hoc, architecture-agnostic counterfactual interpretability layer to a multi-series WaveNet forecaster trained on the full Corporacion Favorita grocery dataset (174,685 series over 1,688 days). The method decomposes each forecast into contributions that sum exactly to the predicted value, avoiding the allocation artifacts we observed with additive SHAP-style attribution. We evaluate faithfulness with a deletion/insertion protocol and find a statistically significant effect on both tests (deletion gap 0.22, p<0.001; insertion gap 0.27, p<0.01; robust across five background-sampling seeds), establishing that the attributions reflect genuine model behavior rather than plausible-looking artifacts. We then characterize, honestly, where attribution is and is not informative: reliance on the promotion signal is heterogeneous across series (median ratio approximately 1.0, with roughly 20% of series showing a strong effect), and the model captures the shape of the weekly sales cycle (day-of-week r=0.78) while systematically under-predicting its amplitude. Our contribution is not improved accuracy but an interpretability layer with a rigorous faithfulness evaluation and a candid account of its limits.

</details>


### [294] [Federated Attack Campaign Detection via Contrastive Encoding of Threat Indicators in Gradient Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04815)
*Manuel Röder,Bibin Babu,Frank-Michael Schleif*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一个名为 FedIoC 的模块化联邦学习框架，用于在跨组织环境下检测协同网络攻击活动。各客户端仅使用本地遥测数据中的结构化威胁指标（IoC）训练共享检测器，无需直接共享敏感情报。方法的核心是：客户端使用对比损失将匹配已知IoC的数据流在嵌入空间中聚拢，使得同一声明战役相关的梯度分量在各客户端间对齐；服务器聚类这些梯度的余弦相似度即可恢复全局战役模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统检测跨组织的协同网络攻击需要跨机构和跨国界共享敏感遥测数据与威胁情报，这存在隐私和合规障碍。联邦学习虽可移除这一屏障（在本地数据上训练共享检测器），但如何在不直接传输IoC的前提下识别出跨组织同一攻击战役的协同模式仍是一个未解问题，需要新的框架和方法。

Method: FedIoC框架：1) 客户端侧编码器使用监督对比损失（supervised contrastive loss），在每个训练批次中将匹配已知IoC模式的数据流作为正样本集，拉近其嵌入、推远非IoC嵌入；2) 由于共享同一攻击战役指标的各客户端产生对齐的梯度分量，服务器通过聚类梯度更新的余弦相似度来恢复全局战役群体；3) 整个过程不直接传输任何IoC数据。

Result: 在两个公开威胁检测基准上进行了评估，设置中每个FL客户端只观察到每个活跃战役的片段且持有来自本地遥测的不相交IoC集。实验表明在此场景下FL服务器能够直接从梯度几何中恢复跨组织的战役群体，并定位了非独立同分布（non-IID）的梯度结构是恢复效果的主要驱动力。

Conclusion: FedIoC作为该场景下的模块化框架被提出，同时论文明确了开放问题：设计优于当前方法中non-IID梯度结构的编码器，以进一步提升跨组织战役恢复能力。

Abstract: Detecting orchestrated cyberattack campaigns that span multiple organizations traditionally requires sharing sensitive telemetry and threat intelligence across institutional boundaries and country borders, a barrier that Federated Learning removes by training shared threat detectors directly on local data. We propose FedIoC, a modular framework in which clients fold locally available structured threat indicators into their gradient updates; we instantiate the client-side encoder with a supervised contrastive loss over IoC-matched flows. Within each training batch, flows that match any known indicator pattern form the positive set; the contrastive objective pulls their learned embeddings together and pushes non-IoC embeddings away, so that campaign-relevant structure is, by design, expressed in the gradient direction. Clients sharing indicators for the same attack campaign then produce aligned gradient components, which the server clusters by the cosine similarity of their updates to recover global campaign patterns without any direct IoC transmission. We evaluate FedIoC on two public threat-detection benchmarks distributed across FL clients that each observe only a fragment of every active campaign and hold disjoint indicator sets derived from their local telemetry. In this regime the FL server recovers cross-organizational campaign cohorts directly from gradient geometry. We contribute FedIoC as a modular framework for this setting, and use it to pinpoint the non-IID gradient structure as the main driver of recovery and to define the open problem of designing encoders that improve on it.

</details>


### [295] [Communication-Efficient Personalized Federated Learning via Layer-Wise Multi-Threshold Random Sketching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04830)
*Xu Zhang,Xingyu Hou,Jiacheng Cheng,Kaiyuan Feng,Maoguo Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于逐层多阈值随机草图的通信高效个性化联邦学习框架，通过为每层分配独立的量化阈值集合并使用多区间低比特压缩，解决了现有单比特方法忽略层间参数分布差异和仅能提供粗糙二值信息的问题，在双向通信中改善了通信-精度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化联邦学习虽能处理数据异质性，但在带宽受限系统中交换高维参数带来大量上下行通信开销。现有单比特压缩方法对整个模型使用单一阈值规则，存在两个局限：一是忽略各层参数分布和量化敏感性的差异；二是单一阈值只能提供粗糙的二进制信息，无法捕捉参数分布的细微变化。

Method: 提出层间多阈值随机草图（layer-wise multi-threshold random sketching）框架：每个层分配独立的量化阈值集合，使压缩表示能适应层特定的统计特性；同时使用多个量化区间对草图参数提供更精细的低比特描述。该方法支持使用紧凑低比特草图的上下行双向通信。

Result: 与现有单比特压缩方相比，所提方法改善了通信-精度权衡（communication-accuracy tradeoff）。

Conclusion: 通过层自适应多阈值量化草图的方案，能够更有效地平衡通信成本与模型精度，为带宽受限场景下的个性化联邦学习提供了一种更优的压缩通信策略。

Abstract: Personalized federated learning (PFL) is a promising paradigm for collaborative learning over distributed devices, where edge nodes collaboratively train personalized models without sharing raw data. Although PFL addresses data heterogeneity by learning client-specific models, it still suffers from substantial uplink and downlink communication costs when exchanging high-dimensional parameters in bandwidth-constrained systems. Recent one-bit methods achieve extreme compression, but they usually rely on a single thresholding rule applied to the whole model. This design has two limitations. First, it overlooks layer-wise differences in parameter distributions and quantization sensitivities. Second, a single threshold provides only coarse binary information and cannot capture fine-grained variations in parameter distributions. To address these issues, we propose a communication-efficient PFL framework via layer-wise multi-threshold random sketching. In the proposed method, each layer is assigned its own set of quantization thresholds, so that the compressed representation can adapt to layer-specific statistics while using multiple intervals to provide a finer low-bit description of sketched parameters. The proposed method supports bidirectional communication using compact low-bit sketches and improves the communication-accuracy tradeoff compared with existing one-bit compression approaches.

</details>


### [296] [PACE: Propagation-Aware Collaborative Correction for One-Shot Personalized Federated Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04832)
*Ruizhe Huang,Chengran Li,Xiaochuan Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PACE框架，用于单轮个性化联邦图学习。PACE将协作知识视为对完整本地预测器的紧凑修正，而非替代品。每个客户端上传秩-r修正载体和对角线消息矩草图，服务器构建传播感知的接收端锚定修正，客户端保留完整本地模型。通过置信负对数似然校准(CNLL)选择本地与外部logits之间的系数。实验表明，排名6时个性化返回仅占密集张量字节的9.6-17.6%，在五个数据集上提升准确率和加权F1，在ogbn-arxiv上正确地将修正权重设为零以完全保留本地预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 客户端异质性在个性化联邦图学习中既是机遇也是风险：其他子图的知识可能补充接收者的本地模型，但不兼容的迁移可能覆盖可靠预测。单轮通信加剧这一矛盾，因为不适合的服务器返回无法事后纠正。现有方法将协作知识视为本地模型的替代品，缺乏对接收端证据的自适应判断机制。

Method: PACE框架包含三个核心设计：(1)客户端上传秩-r修正载体和传播消息矩的对角线草图；(2)服务器构建传播感知的、接收端锚定的修正；(3)接收端保留完整本地模型，通过凸负对数似然校准(CNLL)在验证节点上选择本地与外部logits之间的单一系数，模型参数固定且无需反馈。

Result: 在六个评估数据集上，秩6的个性化返回仅占用9.6-17.6%的密集张量字节。修正获得非零权重，在五个数据集上同时提升准确率和加权F1超过本地模型；在ogbn-arxiv上CNLL将修正的预测权重设为零，精确保留本地预测。对三个引文数据集上的匹配基线应用相同CNLL规则无法解释这些增益。

Conclusion: 核心结论是：一个紧凑传输的修正可以在接收端证据支持时增强完整的本地模型，否则保持本地预测不变。这种接收端锚定的设计能够在单轮通信下平衡外部协作知识的利用与本地可靠预测的保护。

Abstract: Client heterogeneity creates both an opportunity and a risk in personalized federated graph learning. Knowledge held by other subgraphs may complement a receiver's Local model, but an incompatible transfer can override reliable predictions. One-shot communication sharpens this tension because an unsuitable server return cannot be corrected later. We introduce PACE, which treats collaborative knowledge as a compact correction to a complete Local predictor rather than as its replacement. Each client uploads a rank-r update carrier and a diagonal sketch of propagated message moments. The server uses them to construct a propagation-aware, receiver-anchored correction, while the receiver retains its full Local model. Convex negative-log-likelihood calibration (CNLL) then selects one coefficient between Local and External logits using validation nodes; model parameters remain fixed and no feedback is sent. At Rank-6, personalized returns occupy 9.6-17.6% of dense tensor bytes across the six evaluated datasets. The correction receives nonzero weight and improves both Accuracy and weighted-F1 over Local on five datasets; on ogbn-arxiv, CNLL assigns zero predictive weight to the correction and preserves Local predictions exactly. Applying the same CNLL rule to matched baselines on three citation datasets does not account for these gains. The central result is therefore that a small transported correction can augment a complete Local model when receiver evidence supports it while leaving the Local prediction unchanged otherwise.

</details>


### [297] [KVMem: Virtualizing Million-Token Agent Workspaces on a Consumer GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04852)
*Di Chai,Leye Wang,Zeshen Su,Zhiguo Xia,Zhihang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KVMem是一个KV上下文虚拟化系统，将超出GPU KV容量和模型原生上下文窗口的Agent工作区历史以分页KV状态形式保存在GPU内存、主机内存和NVMe存储中。它利用轻量的模型原生注意力空间索引选择相关历史块，并物化一个受限于模型原生上下文窗口的查询相关执行视图。在长达一百万token的长上下文Agent基准测试中，KVMem相比压缩方法（处理上下文溢出的事实标准）取得了更高的任务效用和推理效率。在DeepSWE长上下文测试中，KVMem将任务成功率从43.8%提升到48.4%，并能在配备24GB RTX 5090 Laptop GPU的笔记本上以约50 tokens/s的速度虚拟化高达1M token的Agent工作区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM Agent运行在持续累积的持久工作区中，其历史超过GPU KV容量和模型原生上下文窗口。现有系统通常将旧上下文压缩为摘要或之后以文本形式检索，要么丢失细粒度的执行证据，要么重复预填充模型已处理过的内容。这促使需要一种能够保留完整执行证据并避免重复预填充的方案，同时解耦可寻址工作区大小与模型的原始上下文窗口。

Method: KVMem采用KV上下文虚拟化架构：将溢出的工作区历史以分页KV状态保存，分布于GPU内存、主机内存和NVMe三层存储层级。系统使用轻量的模型原生注意力空间索引来选择相关的历史KV块，并基于查询物化一个执行视图，该视图大小受限于模型的原始上下文窗口，从而同时实现细粒度证据保留和高推理效率。

Result: 在LongMemEval、MemoryAgentBench、AgentLongBench等长达百万token的长上下文基准上，KVMem普遍优于基于压缩的方法。在DeepSWE长上下文测试中（Qwen3.8-27B），任务成功率从压缩方法的43.8%提升至48.4%。本地部署中，KVMem使Qwen3.6/3.8-27B NVFP4配合MTP在配备24GB显存的RTX 5090 Laptop GPU上运行，可虚拟化高达1M token的工作区（为原生256K窗口的4倍），单会话生成速度约50 tokens/s。

Conclusion: 通过将可寻址工作区大小与LLM原生上下文窗口解耦，KVMem为长期运行、工作区可超出原生窗口的持续Agent提供了实用路径，在保证细粒度执行证据的同时实现了交互级推理响应。

Abstract: Modern LLM agents operate in persistent workspaces whose accumulated history can exceed both GPU KV capacity and the model's native context window. Existing systems typically compact older context into summaries or retrieve it later as text, either losing fine-grained execution evidence or repeatedly prefilling content that the model has already processed. We present KVMem, a KV-context virtualization system that preserves overflowed workspace history as paged KV state across GPU memory, host memory, and NVMe. KVMem uses lightweight, model-native attention-space indexes to select relevant historical blocks and materializes a query-dependent execution view bounded by the model's native context window. Extensive evaluations on long-context agent benchmarks spanning histories up to one million tokens, including LongMemEval, MemoryAgentBench, and AgentLongBench, show that KVMem generally achieves higher task utility and greater inference efficiency than compaction-based approaches, the de facto standard for handling context overflow. In the DeepSWE long-context test with Qwen3.8-27B, KVMem improves task success from 43.8% with compaction-only context management to 48.4%.
  In our local-deployment evaluation, KVMem runs Qwen3.6/3.8-27B NVFP4 with MTP on an off-the-shelf laptop equipped with a 24\,GB RTX 5090 Laptop GPU, virtualizing agent workspaces of up to 1M tokens-four times the model's native 256K-token context window. In a single-session setting, KVMem generates $\sim$50 tokens/s, providing interactive responsiveness for local agent execution. More broadly, by decoupling addressable workspace size from the LLM's native context window, KVMem provides a practical path toward long-running agents whose workspaces can grow beyond that window.

</details>


### [298] [When Genomic Masking Priors Fail to Transfer: Strong Variant Prediction, Weak Functional Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04861)
*Susu Hu,Preetam Gattogi,Jens Lehmann,Sahar Vahdati,Stefanie Speidel,Julien Vibert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍GenDA（Genomic Density-optimized Absorbing Diffusion），一个202M参数的双向离散扩散模型，用于基因组建模。经监督微调后，其在ClinVar SNV预测任务上达到0.774 AUROC，超过同规模自回归模型0.103；但熵引导的span放置策略并未带来实际改进，且模型在零样本功能修复压力测试中失败——未能稳定超越保持3-mer组成的对照基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双向离散扩散模型天然适合基因组建模（可从两侧重建缺失序列）。作者假设熵引导的span放置能将重建压力集中在序列组成复杂度高的区域，从而改善下游变异效应预测和功能序列生成。

Method: 开发GenDA模型（202M参数密度优化吸收扩散），采用熵引导的span放置策略进行训练，并通过监督微调用于ClinVar SNV变异效应预测；同时设计零样本功能修复压力测试（涵盖启动子、增强子、外显子边界、内含子边界），并设置控制组（打乱天然gap但精确保留3-mer组成）和匹配随机span变体进行比较。

Result: 微调后的GenDA在ClinVar上AUROC为0.774，比同规模自回归模型高0.103；但匹配随机span变体达到0.777，表明熵引导并非ClinVar改进的原因。更意外的是，GenDA在零样本功能修复测试中失败：在50-500bp gaps下即无法一致超越保留3-mer组成的shuffle对照，增强子退化在较长gaps下加剧。

Conclusion: 作者识别了多个边界条件：熵衡量的是局部序列复杂度而非功能重要性；1-mer tokenization限制了物理上下文；训练span上限300bp不足；高绝对AlphaGenome保真度可与负的对照归一化恢复并存。核心结论是：强微调变异预测、合理损坏先验和功能生成是三个不同主张，需要分别验证。

Abstract: Bidirectional discrete diffusion model appears naturally suited to genomic modeling because it can reconstruct missing sequence from both flanks. We developed GenDA (Genomic Density-optimized Absorbing Diffusion) under the additional hypothesis that entropy-guided span placement would concentrate reconstruction pressure on compositionally complex regions, improving both downstream variant-effect prediction and functional sequence generation. Our results only partially support this premise. After supervised fine-tuning, the 202M-parameter GenDA model reaches a pooled ClinVar SNV AUROC of 0.774, exceeding a similarly scaled autoregressive model by 0.103. However, a matched random-span variant reaches 0.777, providing no evidence that entropy guidance causes the ClinVar improvement. More unexpectedly, GenDA fails a zero-shot functional inpainting stress test: across promoters, enhancers, exon boundaries, and intron boundaries, it does not consistently outperform a control that shuffles the native gap while exactly preserving 3-mer composition. Failure is already present for 50--500-bp gaps, although enhancer degradation worsens at longer gaps. Diagnostics identify several boundary conditions: entropy measures local sequence complexity rather than functional importance; 1-mer tokenization limits physical context; training spans are capped at 300 bp; and high absolute AlphaGenome fidelity can coexist with negative control-normalized restoration. These results show that strong fine-tuned variant prediction, a plausible corruption prior, and functional generation are distinct claims that require separate validation.

</details>


### [299] [From Deep to Shallow: Unconstrained and Efficient Layer Merging Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04881)
*Petro Shulzhenko,Gabriele Spadaro,Enzo Tartaglione*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的深度压缩策略，使得在卷积层中即使使用padding（此前因缺乏解析解而无法合并）也能实现层合并，且不增加合并后卷积核的大小，实测在嵌入式平台上提升推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络计算需求高，在资源受限环境中应用受限。现有深度压缩方法存在两大瓶颈：一是无法直接处理带padding的卷积层合并（缺乏解析解）；二是合并后卷积核尺寸增大，限制了加速收益。

Method: 提出一种高效策略，使得在没有现成解析解的情况下也能实现层合并，并且不增加合并后卷积层的卷积核大小。

Result: 在多种架构和数据集上验证了方法的有效性，并在真实嵌入式平台上测得了推理加速收益。代码已公开开源。

Conclusion: 该方法克服了深度压缩在带padding卷积无法合并以及核尺寸增大两方面的局限，实现了更高效的压缩与推理加速，适用于资源受限的实际部署场景。

Abstract: Although Deep Neural Networks have become foundational in many areas of Machine Learning, high computational demands limit their application in resource-constrained environments. To address this issue, depth compression methods have been proposed to identify and linearize redundant activation functions, thereby allowing for the merging of layers without intermediate non-linearities. However, these methods face two key challenges: they cannot be directly applied to convolutions with padding due to the absence of an analytical solution for merging these layers, and they typically increase the kernel size of merged layers, thus limiting speed-up gains. To overcome these limitations, we propose an efficient strategy that enables merging of layers without an existing analytical solution, and also without increasing kernel size. We validate our approach across multiple architectures and datasets, and measure inference speed-up gains on real embedded platforms. We publicly released the code at https://github.com/ShulzhenkoPetr/deep-to-shallow.

</details>


### [300] [Adaptation Interfaces for In-Context Tabular Foundation Models in Time-to-Event Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04901)
*Minh-Khoi Pham,Luca Cotugno,Dan Cernei,Alina Sirbu,Stefano Masi,Giuseppe Prencipe,Alessandro Pingitore,Patrizia Landi,Working Group on Uric Acid,Cardiovascular Risk of the Italian Society of Hypertension,Tai Tan Mai,Martin Crane,Marija Bezbradica*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在先前工作的基础上，系统研究将表格基础模型（TabFMs）扩展到删失时间-事件预测（生存分析）的方法，通过连接TabFMs与CoxPH、DeepHit等生存接口并修订上下文重采样训练过程，在74个单风险数据集和4个竞争风险数据集上评估了零样本推理、分类微调和生存头适配三种策略，结果表明有效的TabFM迁移取决于数据规模与适配接口的统计结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型在标准分类和回归任务上表现出色，但将其扩展到删失时间-事件预测具有挑战性，因为需要正确处理删失机制和事件时间动态。现有工作缺乏对TabFM在生存分析场景中系统性的评估与适配方法研究。

Method: 在先前工作的基础上，将TabFMs与CoxPH和DeepHit连接，并修订了上下文重采样训练程序。采用冻结的TabFM骨干网络，评估三种策略：时间零样本重构、基于分类的微调、生存头适配，分别在74个单风险数据集和4个竞争风险数据集上进行实验。

Result: 零样本推理在较小的单风险数据集上有效；随着数据规模增大，监督适配的优势愈发明显。Cox作为接口在大数据集上对整合Brier分数（IBS）表现最稳定最佳；DeepHit在时间依赖一致性指数（C-index）上相对强于IBS；竞争风险分析中，因别MTLR在四种TabFM生存头中排名最高。分类微调随数据增长更具竞争力，但在概率预测方面仍较弱。

Conclusion: 有效的TabFM迁移取决于数据规模区间以及所选择适配接口所代表的统计结构，不存在一种普遍最优的适配方法，需要根据具体数据环境选择合适的生存分析接口。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TabFMs) achieve strong performance on structured data, particularly for standard classification and regression problems. Yet, extending them to censored time-to-event prediction is challenging because it requires properly handling censoring and event-time dynamics. Building on our prior work, we further link TabFMs with CoxPH and DeepHit and revise the context-resampled training procedure. We evaluate temporal zero-shot reformulation, classification-based fine-tuning, and survival-head adaptation using frozen TabFM backbones on 74 single-risk data sets, and we additionally study 4 competing-risk data sets. Zero-shot inference is effective on smaller single-risk data sets, whereas supervised adaptation becomes increasingly advantageous as data sets scale. Cox provides the most reliably strong interface, especially for Integrated Brier Score (IBS) on larger data sets. DeepHit is relatively stronger for the time-dependent Concordance Index than for IBS, while cause-specific MTLR ranks highest among the TabFM survival heads in the four-data-set competing-risk analysis. Classification fine-tuning becomes more competitive with zero-shot inference as data sets grow but remains weaker for probabilistic prediction. Overall, our results indicate that effective TabFM transfer depends on the data regime and on the statistical structure represented by the chosen adaptation interface. The implementation scripts used for this work are available at https://github.com/kaylode/survival-fm.

</details>


### [301] [Fast Gauss Sums via Flash Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04910)
*Nicolaj Rux,Sebastian Neumayer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用Flash Attention高效计算含任意符号权重的高斯核和的方法，通过两次小的输入增强将归一化的softmax归约转换为非归一化的高斯和，无需编写任何自定义GPU代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯核和是MMD、核梯度流、SVGD等众多核方法的计算核心，但缺乏像Flash Attention那样经过硬件优化的高效实现。作者希望利用已有的、经过大量硬件工程优化的Flash Attention来加速高斯核和的计算。

Method: 通过对输入进行两种小型增强（augmentations），将归一化的softmax归约转化为未归一化的高斯和计算。这样可以直接复用Flash Attention的高效实现，无需编写自定义GPU内核。

Result: 在特征维度D>8且使用fp16精度时，该方法在速度、内存开销和精度上均优于编译的PyTorch代码和PyKeOps内核（通常显著更优），且内存扩展保持线性。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了成熟的高性能注意力实现（Flash Attention）可以有效地复用于其他核心核方法计算，在保持线性内存开销的同时获得显著的性能优势。

Abstract: Gaussian kernel sums are the computational core of maximum mean discrepancies (MMDs), kernel gradient flows, Stein variational gradient descent (SVGD), and many other kernel methods. At the same time, softmax attention has received an extraordinary amount of hardware-aware code engineering, culminating in flash attention. We show that Gauss kernel sums with arbitrary, signed weights can be evaluated via flash attention: two small input augmentations turn the normalized softmax reduction into the unnormalized Gauss sum, without writing a single line of custom GPU code. For feature dimension D>8 in fp16, this approach beats compiled PyTorch code as well as PyKeOps kernels (often significantly) in speed, memory-overhead and accuracy. Indeed, its memory scaling remains linear.

</details>


### [302] [Physics-Aware Random Walk Fingerprints for Scalable Power Grid Graph Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04943)
*Adnan Anwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MC-PA-RWF（多通道物理感知随机游走指纹）框架，一种用于电力系统级联故障分类的轻量级图级表示方法。该方法将物理边状态引入随机游走传播，通过多个边加权通道提取通道特定指纹并拼接为紧凑表示。在三个PowerGraph基准系统上的实验表明，该方法显著优于仅基于拓扑的RWF，并在最大规模设置下与强GNN基线（GCN、GAT、GINE、TransformerConv）相当甚至更优，平衡准确率达98.04%-99.32%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有级联故障分类任务中，GNN虽预测性能强，但需要端到端训练和模型特定调优，且潜在表示难以对应物理传播模式。随机游走指纹（RWF）虽可扩展且可解释，但现有变体主要强调拓扑和节点级信息，忽视了电网运行相关的边状态信息在游走动态中的作用。

Method: 该方法从域相关属性构建多个边加权通道，从每个加权图中提取通道特定指纹，再将所得向量拼接成紧凑的图级表示。还提出了MC-PA-RWF+节点-边扩展版本，以融合节点与边的信息。

Result: 在三个PowerGraph基准系统上的实验显示，MC-PA-RWF在拓扑仅RWF基础上有显著改进，与强GNN基线（GCN、GAT、GINE、TransformerConv）相比具有竞争力的平衡准确率。在最大评估设置下，MC-PA-RWF+达到约98.04%-99.32%的平衡准确率，失效类F1较最强GNN基线提升1.60-5.84个百分点，且在三个系统上均有统计显著增益。

Conclusion: MC-PA-RWF为电力系统级联故障分类提供了一种可扩展、可解释且性能与GNN相当的替代方案，无需端到端训练和模型特定调优，同时成功将物理边状态纳入随机游走动态中，弥补了传统RWF在电力领域应用中的关键不足。

Abstract: Recent benchmarks such as PowerGraph provide large collections of power-grid graphs for cascading-failure classification. Graph neural networks (GNNs) achieve strong predictive performance on this task, but typically require end-to-end training and model-specific tuning, while their latent representations can be difficult to relate to physically meaningful propagation patterns. Random Walk Fingerprints (RWF) offer a scalable and interpretable alternative, but existing variants primarily emphasise topology and node-level information, leaving grid-relevant operational edge states in the walk dynamics. We propose Multi-Channel Physics-Aware Random Walk Fingerprints (MC-PA-RWF) for power systems, a lightweight graph-level representation framework that introduces physical edge states into random-walk propagation. The method constructs multiple edge-weighted channels from domain-relevant attributes, extracts a channel-specific fingerprint from each weighted graph, and concatenates the resulting vectors into a compact representation. Experiments on three \textit{PowerGraph} benchmark systems show substantial improvements over topology-only RWF and competitive balanced accuracy against strong GNN baselines, including Graph Convolutional Networks (GCN), Graph Attention Networks (GAT), Graph Isomorphism Networks with edge features (GINE), and Transformer-based Graph Convolutional Networks (TransformerConv). At the largest evaluated settings, the node-edge extension MC-PA-RWF+ achieves around 98.04% - 99.32% balanced accuracy and improves failure-class F1 over the strongest GNN baseline by 1.60 -- 5.84 percentage points, with statistically significant gains across all three systems.

</details>


### [303] [Fractal basins trap latent reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04963)
*Jeffrey Lai,Anthony Bao,John Quinn,William Gilpin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现前沿推理模型（如数学定理求解、软件工程、自主任务规划）中存在瞬态混沌现象——一种源自难题计算复杂度的物理后果。研究表明推理模型是具有分形边界的动力系统，且分形程度随任务难度增加而上升；瞬态混沌源于推理在鞍点附近长时间停滞，这些鞍点对应近似正确的解题尝试。这揭示了推理减速是问题难度的必然结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型广泛观察到在更难的题目上推理时间更长，但其背后的普适机制尚未明确。作者旨在揭示推理减速的深层物理/动力学根源，这将有助于理解现代AI推理能力的本质边界。

Method: 作者将推理模型的推理痕迹视为动力系统进行分析：1）在Sudoku、迷宫求解、视觉谜题和数学逻辑等多样化任务上，测量推理模型的动力学行为；2）通过验证分形边界的存在性及分形维度随任务难度的变化，建立瞬态混沌的证据；3）识别推理在鞍点附近的长时间停留，并将鞍点解释为近似正确的解题尝试。

Result: 多种领先推理模型被证实是带有分形吸引域的动力系统；分形维度随任务难度单调增加；瞬态混沌源于模型推理在对应近似正确解的鞍点附近的长时间停滞。

Conclusion: 推理减速并非工程缺陷，而是问题计算复杂度的不可避免的动力学结果。推理痕迹构成了一个丰富的新型动力系统类别，为理解和预测AI推理行为提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Reasoning allows artificial intelligence models to revisit and correct their mistakes, enabling recent frontier advances in mathematical theorem solving, software engineering, and autonomous task planning. Reasoning models are widely observed to reason for longer on harder tasks, but the general mechanism responsible for these slowdowns is unknown. Here, we show that reasoning models exhibit transient chaos, a physical consequence of the computational complexity of difficult tasks. As a consequence, we show that diverse leading reasoning models are dynamical systems with fractal basins, with fractality increasing with task difficulty across diverse tasks like Sudoku and maze solving, visual puzzles, and mathematical logic. We show that transient chaos emerges due to reasoning becoming trapped for extended durations near saddle points, which we show correspond to nearly-correct attempted solutions of the underlying problem. Our results show that reasoning slowdowns are an inevitable consequence of problem hardness in modern artificial intelligence models, and establish reasoning traces as a rich new class of dynamical system.

</details>


### [304] [BeaconKV: Key-Value Cache Compression Guided by Beacon Queries for Efficient Large Reasoning Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04971)
*Janghyeon Kim,Minsoo Kim,Kyuhong Shim,Jungwook Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BeaconKV，一种无需训练的KV缓存压缩方法，用于解决大型推理模型（LRM）在长链条推理中KV缓存内存瓶颈问题。通过发现"想法回溯令牌"（TRT）在嵌入空间中聚类的洞察，BeaconKV维护全局查询聚类的紧凑代表（beacon queries）来预判哪些KV对会被重新访问，在多种推理基准上实现高达5.8倍的内存压缩和4.3倍的吞吐量提升，同时几乎保持完整缓存精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型通过扩展思维链（CoT）获得更强的推理能力，但KV缓存随序列长度线性增长，在长推理轨迹中极易超出GPU内存容量。现有KV缓存压缩方法依赖近期查询作为未来注意力模式的代理，但这一假设在长时序推理中失效——某些解码步骤会重新关注早期上下文（如任务计划），导致现有方法无法准确预测哪些KV对会被重新访问。

Method: 首先通过系统分析发现，对应思想回溯令牌（TRT）的查询在嵌入空间中聚集成少量相似性群组。基于此洞察，BeaconKV维护beacon queries——每个全局查询簇的紧凑代表——以此预判哪些KV对被重新访问，而无需存储整个查询历史。该方法无需训练，直接应用于已有LRM。

Result: 在四个开源大型推理模型和多种推理基准上，BeaconKV整体优于现有压缩方法，实现高达5.8倍的内存压缩，同时几乎保持完整缓存的准确率，并将吞吐量提升超过4.3倍。

Conclusion: BeaconKV通过利用TRT查询的聚类结构，有效解决了长推理链中KV缓存压缩的准确性瓶颈问题，证明了"最近查询即未来重要性代理"这一假设的局限性，并提供了一种无需训练的、高效的内存压缩替代方案，具有显著的实用价值。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) achieve superior problem-solving through extended Chain-of-Thought (CoT) generation, but the resulting key-value (KV) cache grows linearly with sequence length and creates severe memory bottlenecks, often exceeding GPU capacity for long reasoning traces. Existing KV cache compression methods rely on recent queries to estimate future token importance, implicitly assuming these serve as reliable proxies for future attention patterns. We demonstrate that this assumption fails in long-horizon reasoning: certain decoding steps generate Thought Revisiting Tokens (TRT) that re-attend to distant previous context, such as task-solving plans formulated early in the trace. Through systematic analysis, we discover that queries corresponding to the TRT cluster into a small number of similarity groups in the embedding space. Based on this insight, we propose BeaconKV, a training-free KV cache compression method that maintains beacon queries, compact representatives for each global query cluster, to anticipate which KV pairs will be revisited without storing the entire query history. Across four open-source LRMs and diverse reasoning benchmarks, BeaconKV generally outperforms existing compression methods, achieving up to $5.8\times$ memory reduction while nearly preserving full cache accuracy and improving throughput by over $4.3\times$.

</details>


### [305] [Beyond Homoscedasticity: Decoupled Uncertainty Optimization for Deep Imbalanced Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04995)
*Juncheng Zhou,Jiaxi Lu,Weijing Zeng,Zhong Li,Hao Qi,Jingsong Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DUO框架，一种针对深度不平衡回归任务的感知不确定性的长尾回归方法。DUO将回归目标建模为条件高斯分布以刻画实例级预测不确定性，通过解耦均值-方差优化将不确定性转化为尾样本的动态增强信号，并设计分布引导的对比学习机制缓解特征松散和跨标签语义纠缠问题。在IMDB-WIKI-DIR、AgeDB-DIR和AAV2-DIR等视觉与生物基准上取得了最优的few-shot bMAE和GM性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度不平衡回归任务中，现有方法大多通过MSE或其变体学习确定性点映射，隐式假设所有样本具有统一的不确定性水平，忽略了长尾数据中普遍存在的实例级异方差性。此外，异方差的负对数似然在DIR场景下存在梯度耦合问题，削弱了困难尾部样本的学习信号，导致优化惯性和尾部欠拟合。

Method: DUO框架包含三个核心组件：(1)将回归目标建模为条件高斯分布，显式刻画实例级预测不确定性；(2)通过解耦均值-方差优化，将不确定性转化为尾部样本的动态增强信号；(3)设计分布引导的对比学习机制，基于样本分布重叠度自适应构建正负样本对，缓解特征松散和跨标签语义纠缠。

Result: 在视觉和生物DIR基准上（IMDB-WIKI-DIR、AgeDB-DIR和AAV2-DIR），DUO取得了最优的few-shot bMAE和GM指标，同时在few-shot MAE上也保持竞争力。

Conclusion: DUO通过显式建模实例级不确定性并将其转化为尾部样本的动态增强信号，有效解决了DIR中不确定性被忽视和梯度耦合导致的尾样本欠拟合问题，在多项基准上验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Deep Imbalanced Regression (DIR) is pervasive in continuous prediction tasks across diverse modalities, such as age estimation, depth prediction, and protein mutation activity prediction, where label-scarce tail samples often carry higher practical value. However, most existing methods still learn deterministic point mappings under mean squared error or its simple variants, implicitly assuming a uniform uncertainty level across all samples and thereby overlooking the instance-wise heteroscedasticity that is widespread in long-tailed data. We further point out that even heteroscedastic negative log-likelihood suffers from a gradient coupling issue, which, under DIR scenarios, weakens the learning signal of hard tail samples and leads to optimization inertia as well as tail underfitting. To address this, we propose DUO, an uncertainty-aware long-tailed regression framework. Specifically, the proposed method models the regression target as a conditional Gaussian distribution to explicitly characterize instance-level predictive uncertainty, and transforms uncertainty into a dynamic enhancement signal for tail samples through decoupled mean-variance optimization. Furthermore, we design a distribution-guided contrastive learning mechanism that adaptively constructs positive and negative pairs based on the overlap between sample distributions, thereby alleviating feature looseness and cross-label semantic entanglement. Across visual and biological DIR benchmarks, DUO achieves the best few-shot bMAE and GM on IMDB-WIKI-DIR, AgeDB-DIR, and AAV2-DIR while remaining competitive on few-shot MAE.

</details>


### [306] [Solution-space heterogeneity shapes federated learning dynamics across partial differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05012)
*Ping Luo,Jiahuan Wang,Ziqing Wen,Tao Sun,Dongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出solution-space PDE-Dirichlet协议，通过最优传输在解空间分箱上量化联邦PDE学习中客户端之间非独立同分布（non-IID）数据的分布差异，并建立Dirichlet浓度与参数散度之间的理论关系。实验覆盖七类PDE任务、三种神经算子族和五个随机种子，验证了低浓度导致更显著的解距离与优化异构性，且性能退化幅度因任务而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦科学机器学习允许机构在不集中本地物理数据的情况下训练神经代理，但已有的PDE联邦研究缺乏可迁移的non-IID定义。现有协议按方程特定规则划分坐标、系数、边界条件或几何，缺少通用且可量化的数据异构性度量标准，阻碍了不同方法与任务间的公平比较。

Method: 提出solution-space PDE-Dirichlet协议：1）将连续的监督响应转换为可复用的解分箱（solution bins），2）通过解分箱几何上的最优传输（optimal transport）量化客户端间的实际分离程度，3）推导总体分配异质性（population allocation heterogeneity）与Dirichlet浓度之间的精确逆关系。该协议不依赖特定PDE方程形式，具有通用性和可迁移性。

Result: 在7个受控和公开PDE任务、3种神经算子族和5个随机种子下，更低的Dirichlet浓度一致地增大实现的解距离与优化异质性。任务不同的性能退化表现：最大效应出现在低粘度Burgers方程，最异构设置下最终误差相差4.157个百分点；额外通信或更平滑的动力学可缩小最终性能差距，尽管参数分离仍然持续存在。

Conclusion: 研究区分了可复现的几何机制（几何层面的数据分离）与依赖任务的泛化结果，为评估non-IID联邦PDE学习提供了统一基础，并揭示参数层面的持续分离并不必然等同于最终性能的显著退化。

Abstract: Federated scientific machine learning enables institutions to train neural surrogates without centralizing local physical data, yet studies of partial differential equations (PDEs) lack a transferable definition of non-independent and identically distributed data. Existing protocols partition coordinates, coefficients, boundary conditions, or geometries according to equation-specific rules. Here, we introduce solution-space PDE-Dirichlet, a protocol that converts continuous supervised responses into reusable solution bins and quantifies the realized separation between clients through optimal transport over the geometry of these bins. We derive an exact inverse relation between population allocation heterogeneity and the Dirichlet concentration, and we establish conditions under which response heterogeneity induces gradient disagreement, local-update dispersion, and parameter divergence. Across seven controlled and public PDE tasks, three neural-operator families, and five random seeds, a lower concentration consistently increases the realized solution distance and optimization heterogeneity. The degradation in final error is task dependent: the largest effect occurs for low-viscosity Burgers, reaching 4.157 percentage points under the most heterogeneous setting, whereas additional communication or smoother dynamics can reduce the final gap despite persistent parameter separation. These results distinguish a reproducible geometric mechanism from task-dependent generalization outcomes and provide a common basis for evaluating non-IID federated PDE learning.

</details>


### [307] [Amortizing Scaling Law Construction Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05016)
*Abhash Kumar Jha,Diana Alexandra Onuţu,Neeratyoy Mallik,Swagatam Haldar,Sam Laing,Niccolò Ajroldi,Shiwei Liu,Joaquin Vanschoren,Aaron Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种高效的规模法则（scaling law）构建框架，将数据收集形式化为贝叶斯优化问题，通过渐进扩大计算预算并利用代理模型的幻想评估来补充观测配置，可在节省10-100倍计算量的情况下，获得与在完整稠密网格上拟合规模法则几乎相同的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推导规模法则通常需要在超参数、token预算和参数数量上进行穷举网格搜索来训练大量模型，计算成本极高。然而，拟合规模法则实际上只需要各计算规模下的最优损失前沿，训练的大多数配置都被丢弃了，存在巨大的计算浪费。

Method: 提出一个高效构建规模法则的框架：(1) 将数据收集过程形式化为贝叶斯优化问题；(2) 提出在受限计算预算下比较不同规模法则拟合方法的度量指标；(3) 在采集过程中渐进扩大计算预算（与实践中按计算量排序评估配置的方式一致），显著提升恢复效率；(4) 用代理模型的幻想（fantasized）评估来增补已观测配置，从而恢复更广泛的实验网格，无需训练每个配置即可准确拟合规模法则。

Result: 所提方法能够在节省高达10到100倍计算量的情况下，紧密匹配在完整稠密网格上拟合的规模法则结果。

Conclusion: 通过将数据收集视为贝叶斯优化并结合渐进式预算扩展与幻想评估补充，可以在大幅降低计算成本的前提下高效、准确地构建规模法则，为大规模基础模型的训练设计提供指导。

Abstract: Scaling laws guide the design choices for training large foundation models, but deriving them involves training an exhaustive grid over hyperparameters, token budgets, and parameter counts, which is computationally expensive. Fitting a scaling law, however, only requires the best-loss frontier across compute scales, discarding most of the trained configurations. We propose a framework for efficient scaling law construction that formulates data collection as a Bayesian optimization problem, and introduce metrics for comparing scaling law fitting methods under constrained compute budgets. We find that progressively expanding the compute budget during acquisition, mirroring the compute-ordered evaluation of configurations in practice, substantially improves recovery efficiency. Augmenting the observed configurations with surrogate-fantasized evaluations then recovers the broader experimental grid, allowing accurate scaling law fitting without training every configuration. Together, these can closely match scaling law fits over a full dense grid at computational savings of up to $10\text{--}100\times$.

</details>


### [308] [Confounding-Valid Conformal Inference for Counterfactual KPIs in Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05073)
*Abdessamed Qchohi,Jessica Moysen Cortes,Matteo Zecchin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CV-CCI方法，通过结合丰富的观测遥测数据与有限的随机遥测数据，实现存在隐藏混杂情况下仍然有效的反事实置信推断，在保证有限样本覆盖保证的同时提升预测集效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络运营商需要利用日志遥测数据进行反事实推断来回答"what-if"问题（如预测集形式的KPI）。然而，日志遥测可能遗漏控制器使用的变量，导致隐藏混杂并使反事实分析的统计保证失效。虽然可以通过随机化遥测解决，但随机化会扰乱正常网络运行，因此随机遥测通常稀缺，仅靠它产生的预测集信息量不足、不够高效。

Method: 提出Confounding-Valid Counterfactual Conformal Inference (CV-CCI)，该方法基于General Synthetic-Powered Inference (GESPI)原则，将大量可能混杂的观测遥测数据与有限的随机化数据相结合。利用观测数据提升效率，同时利用随机化数据在任意隐藏混杂下保留有限样本覆盖保证。

Result: 在两个代表性无线接入网（RAN）控制任务上的实验表明，CV-CCI在隐藏混杂下保持有效性（即覆盖保证有效），同时生成的预测集比现有最先进的混杂有效基线方法更高效（预测集更紧凑、信息量更大）。

Conclusion: CV-CCI成功解决了网络反事实推断中观测数据存在隐藏混杂但数据丰富、随机化数据无混杂但数据稀缺之间的矛盾，在不牺牲统计有效性的前提下显著提升了预测集的效率，为网络运维中的反事实分析提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Conformal counterfactual inference enables network operators to use logged telemetry to reliably answer 'what-if' questions about network operation. These answers typically take the form of prediction sets that contain, with a user-defined probability, the key performance indicators (KPIs) that would have been observed under alternative control actions. A key challenge is that logged telemetry may omit variables used by the controller, resulting in hidden confounding and invalidating the statistical guarantees of counterfactual analysis. In principle, this issue can be addressed using randomized telemetry, collected by assigning control actions independently of the network state. However, because such randomization may disrupt normal operation, randomized telemetry is typically scarce, causing counterfactual analysis based solely on it to produce uninformative prediction sets. To address these challenges, we propose Confounding-Valid Counterfactual Conformal Inference (CV-CCI), which combines abundant, potentially confounded observational telemetry with limited randomized data through the General Synthetic-Powered Inference (GESPI) principle. CV-CCI leverages observational data to improve efficiency while using randomized data to retain finite-sample coverage guarantees under arbitrary hidden confounding. Experiments on two representative radio access network (RAN) control tasks show that CV-CCI remains valid under hidden confounding while producing more efficient prediction sets than state-of-the-art confounding-valid baselines.

</details>


### [309] [Deep Microcompression: Structured Pruning and Bit-packed Quantization for Microcontrollers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05081)
*Opegbemi Matthias Busoye,Tolulope Matthew Busoye,Eghonghon-aye Eigbe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DMC（深度微压缩），一种面向裸机微控制器的硬件感知深度学习推理流水线，集成了结构化剪枝、量化感知训练和定长位打包技术，在LeNet-5上实现55.8倍权重压缩比（98.77%准确率），并首次在仅2KB SRAM的ATmega328P上实现了标准CNN推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 裸机微控制器（如ATmega328P）的资源极其有限（仅2KB SRAM），传统观点认为此类设备无法运行CNN推理。现有方案如TensorFlow Lite Micro仍未能在如此受限的硬件上实现标准CNN部署，因此亟需一种硬件感知的压缩流水线来突破这一瓶颈。

Method: DMC采用三阶段硬件感知流水线：(1) 结构化剪枝去除不重要的权重；(2) 量化感知训练将权重映射到低比特表示；(3) 定长位打包实现高效存储。输出为无依赖的C语言库，保证确定性延迟，并直接在目标MCU硬件上进行端到端优化。

Result: 在LeNet-5上实现55.8倍权重压缩比，准确率达98.77%。在RP2040（Cortex-M0+）上，相比TensorFlow Lite，二进制体积减少3倍且准确率持平。首次在仅2KB SRAM的ATmega328P上成功部署标准CNN，此前被认为在该设备上运行CNN推理是不可行的。

Conclusion: DMC证明了通过硬件感知的压缩设计，即使在资源极度受限的裸机微控制器上也能实现标准CNN的高效推理，大幅拓展了边缘深度学习的部署边界。

Abstract: This paper introduces Deep Microcompression (DMC), a hardware-aware pipeline for deep learning inference on bare-metal microcontrollers. DMC integrates structured pruning, quantization-aware training, and fixed-length bit-packing to achieve a 55.8$\times$ weight compression ratio on LeNet-5 (98.77\% accuracy), generating a dependency-free C library with deterministic latency. On the RP2040 (Cortex-M0+), DMC reduces binary size by 3$\times$ versus TensorFlow Lite while matching its accuracy. Critically, DMC enables the first documented deployment of a standard CNN on the ATmega328P, a device constrained to 2KB SRAM, previously considered infeasible for CNN inference.

</details>


### [310] [NEAT-POCKET: Pocket-Conditioned Autoregressive 3D Molecular Generation with a Neighborhood-Guided Set Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05097)
*Roxane Axel Jacob,Daniel Rose,Thierry Langer,Johannes Kirchmair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NEAT-POCKET是NEAT自回归模型的口袋条件扩展，用于3D分子生成。该模型在蛋白质结合口袋环境中逐原子生成分子，同时保持原子排列不变性并显式建模氢原子。在CrossDocked和SPINDR数据集上的基准测试表明，其结构生成性能具有竞争力，且采样速度显著快于现有基线。此外，该框架天然支持口袋条件下的片段补全，适用于先导化合物优化和骨架扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI驱动的从头分子设计通过直接在靶蛋白结合口袋内生成新颖配体，有望加速早期药物发现。现有方法在口袋条件下的3D分子生成方面存在效率或表现瓶颈，需要一种既快速又灵活的生成框架。

Method: NEAT-POCKET是对自回归NEAT模型的口袋条件扩展。其核心特点包括：在蛋白质口袋环境中逐原子生成分子；保持原子排列不变性；显式建模氢原子。该模型可同时支持全分子生成和口袋条件下的片段补全任务。

Result: 在CrossDocked和SPINDR两个基准数据集上，NEAT-POCKET实现了具有竞争力的基于结构的分子生成性能，同时采样速度显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: NEAT-POCKET被定位为一种快速、灵活且实用的基于结构的药物设计框架，尤其因其天然支持片段补全任务，在先导化合物优化和骨架扩展方面具有直接应用价值。

Abstract: AI-driven de novo molecular design offers a promising route to accelerate early-stage drug discovery by generating novel ligands directly within target protein binding pockets. We present NEAT-POCKET, a pocket-conditioned extension of the autoregressive NEAT model for 3D molecular generation. NEAT-POCKET generates molecules atom by atom in protein pocket environments while preserving atom permutation invariance and explicitly modeling hydrogen atoms. Benchmarks on the CrossDocked and SPINDR datasets show that NEAT-POCKET achieves competitive structure-based generation performance while sampling substantially faster than existing baselines. Beyond full-molecule generation, NEAT-POCKET naturally enables pocket-conditioned fragment completion, a task directly relevant to lead optimization and scaffold elaboration. These results position NEAT-POCKET as a fast, flexible, and practical framework for structure-based drug design.

</details>


### [311] [A Comparative Study of Counterfactual Explainers for Graph Neural Networks Enabling Multiple Types of Graph Edit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05113)
*Maria Myrto Villia,Filippos Gouidis,Theodore Patkos,Panos Trahanias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对六种最先进的图反事实解释模型进行了全面比较研究，涵盖二分类和多分类的图级与节点级分类任务，在多样化的真实与合成数据集上使用定量和定性指标评估其性能，以揭示各方法的优势与不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管支持边增删的图反事实解释方法已出现，但缺乏通用且高效的方法，现有方法在解释规模、覆盖率和质量之间存在权衡，需要在不同场景下系统评估各方法的优劣以指导未来研究。

Method: 在多样化的真实世界和合成数据集上，比较六种最先进的（SOTA）图反事实解释模型，涵盖二分类和多分类的图级与节点级分类任务，并使用多种定量和定性指标进行性能评估。

Result: 研究揭示了六种SOTA模型在不同数据集和任务上的表现差异，指出了每种方法各自的优势与不足，说明了其在解释规模、覆盖率和质量之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该比较研究为图反事实解释领域提供了系统性的评估基准，识别出当前方法的关键局限性和优势场景，为未来研究指明了改进方向。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations for graph-structured data seek to determine minimal and realistic modifications required in an input graph to alter a model's prediction to a predefined output. Although counterfactual explainers that support modifying the graph by both adding and removing edges have recently emerged, there is still a lack of general and efficient methods, especially when considering the quality of the generated explanations. Moreover, the problem remains far from solved, as existing methods exhibit different strengths and weaknesses, often trading off between explanation size, coverage and quality. For this reason, it is important to identify where each method performs well and where it falls short, so as to guide future research in the field. Thus, our study compares six state-of-the-art (SOTA) models on a diverse set of real-world and synthetic datasets, covering both binary and multi-class graph and node classification tasks, and evaluates their performance using diverse quantitative and qualitative metrics.

</details>


### [312] [Single-Query Black-Box Calibration Auditing via Logit Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05125)
*Roman Plaud,Antoine Saillenfest,Matthieu Labeau,Thomas Bonald,Willem Waegeman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用logit_bias参数的黑盒LLM校准评估方法，仅需每样本一次查询即可精确估计二元分类任务的真实校准误差（True Calibration Error），为审计不透明的基础模型提供了高效框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型作为零样本分类器的安全部署依赖于校准评估，但商业API提供商日益隐藏连续输出概率，使得标准校准指标无法计算，亟需一种绕过这种不透明性的审计方法。

Method: 利用任何暴露logit_bias参数的LLM API，通过数学操作构造查询以精确评估概率阈值，从而引入一种新颖且可证明一致的True Calibration Error估计器，仅需每个样本一次查询。

Result: 所提出的估计器在数学上可证明是一致的（provably consistent），能够仅用每样本一次查询即可实现校准误差的可靠估计，为黑盒模型的审计提供了可行手段。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了LLM API的logit_bias参数可被用于高效的黑盒校准审计，为理解和评估不透明基础模型的安全性提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Evaluating the calibration of Large Language Models (LLMs) is critical for their safe deployment as zero-shot classifiers. Yet, commercial API providers increasingly hide the continuous output probabilities required by standard calibration metrics. To bypass this opacity, we demonstrate that any LLM API exposing a logit\_bias parameter can be mathematically manipulated to evaluate exact probability thresholds using strictly one query per sample. Leveraging this mechanism, we introduce a novel and provably consistent estimator of the True Calibration Error for binary tasks. Our approach therefore provides an efficient framework for auditing black-box foundation models.

</details>


### [313] [Coarse-Graining Hidden Representations: Unsupervised Neuron Selection via Mapping Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05126)
*Margherita Mele,Andrea Castagna,Roberto Menichetti,Raffaello Potestio,Alessandro Ingrosso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出利用映射熵（Mapping Entropy, ME）作为无监督标准，对过参数化神经网络中的隐藏层神经元进行粗粒化选择，以识别对任务至关重要的神经元子集，并在教师-学生网络、非线性高斯过程任务和MNIST增强任务上验证了所选子网络的性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过参数化神经网络包含远超任务需求的隐藏单元，这引发了一个核心问题：哪些神经元是本质必要的，以及这种区分是否能在无需标签或梯度的条件下，从表示本身中被识别出来。

Method: 将神经元选择形式化为通过保留神经元子集对隐藏层进行粗粒化的问题；用映射熵（ME）对每个候选选择进行评分，ME衡量丢弃部分网络神经元所导致的判别力损失，最小化ME的选择被视为最具信息量；该标准完全无监督，仅依赖隐藏激活的统计信息。

Result: 在教师-学生网络中，ME优化可恢复最小教师一致表示，并依据隐藏层残余变异性按比例保留额外单元；在非线性高斯过程任务中，它选择了训练期间偏好类别发生漂移的一致功能类映射。在该任务以及翻译增强MNIST上，ME选择的子网络在同等规模下优于随机子集，在强压缩条件下优势最为明显，从而将构型可区分性与预测性能联系起来。

Conclusion: 通过ME进行无监督的神经元选择能够识别有意义的、功能上一致且性能优越的子网络，表明构型可区分性可作为预测表现的可靠指标，所提方法在强压缩场景下尤其有效。

Abstract: Overparameterized neural networks carry far more hidden units than a task nominally requires, raising the question of which neurons are essential and whether that distinction is legible in the representation itself, without labels or gradients. We cast neuron selection as the problem of coarse-graining the hidden layer by retaining a subset of its neurons, and score each putative selection by the mapping entropy (ME). This quantity measures the loss of discriminatory power inherent in discarding part of the network neurons, and the selection that minimises the ME is taken as particularly informative. This criterion is fully unsupervised, in that it depends only on hidden-activation statistics. In teacher-student networks, ME optimisation recovers the minimal teacher-consistent representation and retains extra units in proportion to the hidden layer's residual variability; in a non-linear Gaussian process task, it selects coherent functional-class mappings whose preferred class shifts across training. On this task and on translation-augmented MNIST, ME-selected subnetworks outperform random subsets of equal size, most clearly under strong compression - linking configurational distinguishability to predictive performance.

</details>


### [314] [MomentQuant: an even more minimalist interval method with linear time complexity for time series classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05136)
*Johann Faouzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MomentQuant算法，通过优化Quant的实现（更快）并利用Cornish-Fisher展开计算近似分位数（无需排序，降低计算复杂度），在不显著降低预测性能的前提下大幅提升时间序列分类的推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列分类在真实应用中广泛存在，但现有算法在预测性能与计算成本之间存在权衡。Quant算法已具有高精度和高速度，但在实时/高频推理场景下仍可进一步优化，以减小推理开销（推理频率远高于训练频率）。

Method: 提出两方面的改进：(1) 对Quant算法进行更优化的工程实现，保持算法逻辑不变但提升运行效率；(2) 引入Cornish-Fisher展开来近似分位数，避免对原始时间序列进行排序操作，从而降低计算复杂度。由此衍生出新算法MomentQuant。

Result: 实验证明：(a) 对Quant的重新实现比原始实现更快；(b) MomentQuant比优化后的Quant还要快；(c) MomentQuant的加速只以极小的预测性能下降为代价。

Conclusion: MomentQuant在保持Quant高精度特性的同时显著提升了推理速度，特别适合推理频率远高于训练频率的真实应用场景。通过去除排序步骤降低了计算复杂度，是时间序列分类算法在效率-性能权衡上的有效改进。

Abstract: Time series data is very common in many real-world applications and in numerous domains, with increasing interest for automated information extraction using machine learning. One of these subfields is time series classification, which consists in assigning a label to each new, unseen time series. Many algorithms have been developed over the past decades, with the trade-off between predictive performance and computational cost being consistently discussed. Quant, an interval-based algorithm extracting quantiles from recursive, fixed, dyadic intervals, was shown to achieve high accuracy, while being very fast. We propose two changes to make this algorithm even faster. The first one is a better optimized implementation of the exact same algorithm. The second one is to derive approximate quantiles, using the Cornish-Fisher expansion, instead of exact quantiles. This change removes the necessity to sort the time series, leading to a smaller computational complexity. We call this novel algorithm MomentQuant. We provide evidence that our implementation of Quant is faster than the original one, and that MomentQuant is even faster than our implementation of Quant, at the cost of a tiny decrease in predictive performance. These improvements are especially relevant for real-life applications, where inference is performed much more often than training.

</details>


### [315] [From 80x to 385x: A Best-Matching-Unit Search at the L2 Roof, Measured Against a Symmetrically Tuned Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05138)
*Andrew James Amos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文报告了一个对新型SOM算法（SparseBin）及其基线算法（cuSPARSE）同时进行调优的项目，通过四个调优杠杆将训练速度提升了5.6-10.1倍/epoch，并发现最优内核已达到L2带宽上界的77%，是一个终点性结果而非中间站。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出GPU实现之间的比较通常不对等（一方作者调优、另一方直接运行现成版本），本文旨在建立公平比较，同时对竞争双方进行同等程度的调优。

Method: 对主导自组织映射（SOM）训练的最佳匹配单元搜索过程，通过四个杠杆进行调优：tile size（瓦片大小）、tile-membership clustering（瓦片成员聚类）、neuron-axis chunking（神经元轴分块）以及vectorised loads（向量化加载）。对cuSPARSE也施加了所有可类比对应的杠杆。

Result: 在32x32到512x512的映射尺寸下，每个epoch速度提升5.6-10.1倍（相对先前发表配置）；相对先前MEDLINE图谱所用CUDA实现的优势从约80倍提升到约385倍。调优后的cuSPARSE也提速了2-3倍。最优内核达到L2带宽峰值77%的使用率，其余单元为40-65%，剩余改进空间被约束在约1.3倍以内。

Conclusion: 该结果是一个终端性（terminal）结论而非中间站——所有未测试的杠杆要么被带宽上界结构性封顶，要么被实测为零效果，表明该实现已达到接近硬件极限的优化水平。

Abstract: Comparisons between GPU implementations are usually asymmetric: one side is tuned by its author, the other is run as found. I report a programme that tuned both a novel SOM algorithm (SparseBin) and the baseline algorithm it was being compared to (cuSPARSE). The best-matching-unit search that dominates self-organizing map training was tuned through four levers - tile size, tile-membership clustering, neuron-axis chunking and vectorised loads - reaching 5.6-10.1x per epoch over the previously published configuration at map sizes from 32x32 to 512x512, and lifting the margin over the CUDA implementation behind our earlier MEDLINE atlases from ~80x to ~385x. cuSPARSE, the implementation SparseBin is compared against, received every lever with an analogue on its side, and became 2-3x faster in the process. The tuned kernel pressed the L2 bandwidth roof at 77% of peak with every other unit at 40-65%, bounding any further lever at ~1.3x - a terminal result rather than a waypoint, and every untested lever was either capped by that bound by construction or measured null.

</details>


### [316] [Beyond Stationarity in Time Series: Discovering Causal Structures and Latent Regimes via Markov Blankets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05150)
*Lei Zan,Charles K. Assaad,Emilie Devijver,Eric Gaussier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RCBNB-MB算法，一种用于时间序列的因果发现方法，通过马尔可夫毯识别潜在因果机制（regime），将时间序列分段并在每个段内发现因果图，放宽了传统假设中因果结构随时间不变的约束，并在模拟与真实IT监控数据上验证了优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列通常在离散时间点观测，且现实动态系统中常常存在机制转换（regime changes），这挑战了静态因果结构的假设。传统因果发现方法假设整个时间序列共享单一的、时间一致的因果结构，这在许多现实场景中不成立，因此需要一个能够处理非平稳因果结构的算法。

Method: RCBNB-MB采用迭代策略：首先将时间序列分割为多个因果机制（regime），每个regime定义为具有稳定因果结构的时间点子集；然后在每个regime内部通过马尔可夫毯（Markov blanket）而非直接父节点来发现因果图，从而提升对因果发现误差的鲁棒性并保留预测信息。算法在合理假设下提供了恢复regime转换与因果图的理论保证。

Result: 在具有已知ground truth的模拟数据集和真实世界IT监控数据上的大量实验表明，RCBNB-MB在准确检测regime变化及其关联的因果图方面系统性地优于基线方法。

Conclusion: RCBNB-MB为处理非平稳时间序列的因果发现提供了一个鲁棒且通用的框架，通过在因果发现中利用马尔可夫毯并识别潜在因果机制，有效解决了现实动态系统中因果结构变化的核心挑战。

Abstract: This paper introduces Regime-aware Constraint-Based and Noise-Based causal discovery with Markov Blankets (RCBNB-MB), a novel causal discovery algorithm for time series that relaxes the common assumption of a single, time-consistent causal structure. Time series are typically observed at discrete time points and often exhibit regime changes that challenge the assumption of a static causal structure, a limitation in many real-world dynamic systems. To address this challenge, RCBNB-MB identifies latent causal regimes, defined as subsets of time points within which a stable causal structure holds. The algorithm follows an iterative strategy that segments the time series into regimes and discovers the causal graph within each regime. By leveraging the Markov blanket rather than direct parents, RCBNB-MB gains robustness to errors in causal discovery and preserves predictive information. We provide theoretical guarantees for RCBNB-MB's ability to recover both regime transitions and causal graphs under reasonable assumptions. Furthermore, we validate its effectiveness through extensive experiments on simulated datasets with known ground truth and real-world IT monitoring data, where taking into account regime shifts is critical. Empirical results show that RCBNB-MB systematically outperforms baseline approaches in accurately detecting regime changes and their associated causal graphs, positioning it as a robust and versatile framework for non-stationary time series analysis.

</details>


### [317] [Phase Transition Frequency as a Training Time Predictor of Test Accuracy in ResNets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05194)
*Arunan J*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文实证研究了ResNet微调过程中类别可分性跳跃次数(test accuracy)作为最终测试准确率的预测指标。在75个实验（4个基准、3种架构）中，发现i.i.d.分类基准上跳跃次数与最终准确率呈强负相关（CIFAR-10 r=-0.84, CIFAR-100 r=-0.87），但在分布偏移基准（TinyImageNet, CIFAR-10-C）下相关性减弱。该指标在CIFAR-100上优于六种替代训练曲线信号，且对架构深度具有部分相关性上的稳健性，被提出作为一种分布内训练质量探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找一种便捷、廉价的训练过程信号，能够在训练早期或中间阶段预测模型最终测试准确率，以辅助超参数调优、早停和训练监控，而不需等到训练结束或进行昂贵的推理评估。

Method: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、TinyImageNet和CIFAR-10-C四个基准上，使用ResNet-18/50/101三种架构进行75组实验（每组5-10个种子），实证记录ResNet微调过程中离散的类别可分性跳跃次数，计算其与最终测试准确率的Pearson相关性；通过偏相关（控制架构深度）验证稳健性；并与六种替代训练曲线信号进行相关性对比。

Result: i.i.d.标准基准上跳跃次数与最终准确率强负相关（CIFAR-10 r=-0.84, p<1e-8; CIFAR-100 r=-0.87, p<1e-5）；分布偏移下关系减弱（TinyImageNet r=-0.45, CIFAR-10-C r=-0.19）。控制架构深度后(CIFAR-100 r_partial=-0.69, p=0.007)仍具显著性。与六种替代信号对比中，跳跃次数在CIFAR-100上最强，在CIFAR-10上属最强之一，但在两个压力基准上被其他信号超越。

Conclusion: 离散类别可分性跳跃次数可作为分布内训练质量的廉价探针，但仅在i.i.d.分布内有效；在分布偏移场景下预测力衰减。该指标可作为候选训练探针家族中的一员，而非通用预测器。与有效秩、Hessian尖锐度、Fisher信息、边际及神经坍缩等更强竞争指标的对比留待未来研究。

Abstract: The number of discrete class-separability jumps observed during ResNet finetuning is examined empirically as a predictor of final test accuracy. Across 75 experiments spanning four benchmarks (CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet, and CIFAR-10-C) and three architectures (ResNet-18, ResNet-50, and ResNet-101), with five to ten seeds per configuration, a strong within-dataset negative correlation is obtained on standard i.i.d. classification benchmarks: \(r = -0.84\) on CIFAR-10 (\(p < 10^{-8}\), \(n = 30\)) and \(r = -0.87\) on CIFAR-100 (\(p < 10^{-5}\), \(n = 15\)). Under distributional stress, the relationship attenuates: TinyImageNet yields \(r = -0.45\), and the CIFAR-10-C corruption benchmark yields \(r = -0.19\). Two additional analyses discipline the empirical claim. A partial correlation controlling for architecture depth, treated as a linear covariate, shows that on CIFAR-100 the transition count retains statistically significant predictive power (\(r_{\mathrm{partial}} = -0.69\), \(p = 0.007\)); the corresponding result under the stricter categorical conditioning is not established at \(n = 15\). A comparison against six alternative training-curve signals shows that transition count achieved the strongest correlation among the evaluated signals on CIFAR-100 and one of the strongest on CIFAR-10, but is dominated by other signals on the two stressed benchmarks. The comparison is restricted to training-curve-level signals; comparisons against effective rank, Hessian sharpness, Fisher information, margin, and neural-collapse measures, which are the strongest competitors in the current literature, are not part of the present study and remain open. The observation is presented as an in-distribution training-quality probe among a family of candidate probes, and an inexpensive detection procedure suitable for logging alongside a standard training loop is provided.

</details>


### [318] [Dimension-Adaptive Batched Lipschitz Narrowing Without Knowing the Zooming Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05214)
*Yasong Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Count-Adaptive BLiN算法，消除了A-BLiN序列中对缩放维度d_z的依赖，通过根据前一阶段淘汰后幸存的立方体数量选择下一边缘长度，在不需要d_z和缩放常数C_z的情况下，仍达到与原始算法相同的遗憾界和批次复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始A-BLiN算法中，恰当组合边缘长度(ACE)序列依赖于缩放维度d_z，这要求使用者预先知道d_z这一参数。作者希望消除这一依赖，使算法在d_z未知时仍然能够达到最优的遗憾界和批次复杂度。

Method: 提出Count-Adaptive BLiN算法：下一边缘长度的选择不再依赖d_z，而是根据前一轮消除后幸存的立方体数量来确定。该算法完全不需要d_z或缩放常数C_z的信息。

Result: 该算法在不需要d_z和C_z的情况下，仍然达到Õ_d(T^{(d_z+1)/(d_z+2)})的遗憾界，并使用O_d(log log T)个批次。结合原始论文定理10中的自适应网格下界，当d_z未知时最优批次复杂度仍为Θ_d(log log T)。

Conclusion: 通过基于计数的自适应边缘长度选择策略，成功移除了BLiN算法对d_z参数的依赖，同时保持最优的遗憾界和批次复杂度，解决了d_z未知情形下的最优批次复杂性问题。

Abstract: The Appropriately Combined Edge-length (ACE) sequence in A-BLiN depends on the zooming dimension $d_z$. This note removes that dependence. The next edge length is selected from the number of cubes that survive the preceding elimination. The resulting Count-Adaptive BLiN algorithm does not use $d_z$ or the zooming constant $C_z$, yet it attains $\widetilde{\mathcal O}_d(T^{(d_z+1)/(d_z+2)})$ regret with $\mathcal O_d(\log\log T)$ batches. Together with the adaptive-grid lower bound in Theorem 10 of the original paper, the optimal batch complexity remains $Θ_d(\log\log T)$ when $d_z$ is unknown.

</details>


### [319] [FedDRAW: Federated Dual Reputation Annealing Weighting for Heterogeneous Multi-Institutional Chest Radiograph Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05223)
*Maryam Moradpour,Anne-Christin Hauschild*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedDRAW（联邦双声誉退火加权），一种服务器端聚合方法，将数据量先验与客户端-全局参数余弦相似度结合，通过内外两个退火调度来动态调整联邦学习中的聚合权重，解决FedAvg中样本量主导导致小型但信息量高的医院被边缘化的问题。在12个模拟客户端分区场景、两个胸片数据集上对七个联邦基线进行评测，FedDRAW取得最高平均排名，且经Friedman检验与Nemenyi事后分析确认差异具有统计显著性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习允许医院在数据不出本地的情况下协作训练诊断模型，但FedAvg按样本量比例分配聚合权重，导致小规模但信息丰富的医院始终被赋予低影响力，而大型客户端即使信息量低也可能主导全局模型，造成有偏的诊断模型。

Method: FedDRAW在服务器端将数据量先验与客户端和全局参数间的余弦相似度结合，使用两个耦合的退火调度：内层调度将客户端声誉从数据量先验逐步转向相似度；外层延迟退火调度作用于softmax逆温度，在训练前期和中期保持权重选择性，在收敛时放松至均匀分布。

Result: 在12个模拟客户端分区场景（CheXpert和ChestMNIST两个胸片数据集）中与七个联邦基线（均采用相同本地训练设置）比较，FedDRAW在AUC和灵敏度-特异度几何均值（GM）两项指标下均获得八种方法中的最高平均排名，并通过Friedman检验和Nemenyi事后分析证实方法间差异具有统计学意义。

Conclusion: 通过调度两种信号（数据量先验与参数相似度）而非仅按样本数固定权重，有望构建偏差更小的医学诊断模型。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence models are promising for medical diagnosis, but they require large numbers of unbiased data, which in medicine are distributed across hospitals and cannot be centralized to protect patient privacy. Federated Learning (FL) addresses this, since hospitals train one shared diagnostic model while patient data remain local. Training proceeds in communication rounds, in which each hospital trains the shared model locally and returns it to the server for merging by weighted average. This aggregation weight determines whose institutional knowledge shapes the result. Federated averaging (FedAvg) sets it in proportion to local sample count, so a small but informative hospital is permanently assigned a small influence, andl argest clients could dominate the global model even when they are less informative. We propose Federated Dual Reputation Annealing Weighting (FedDRAW), a server-side aggregation method that combines a data-size prior with the cosine similarity between client and global parameters under two coupled annealing schedules. An inner schedule shifts client reputation from the size prior towards similarity. An outer, deferred annealing schedule on the softmax inverse temperature keeps the weighting selective in the early and middle rounds and relaxes it to uniformity at convergence. We evaluate FedDRAW on 12 simulated client-partition scenarios of two chest radiograph datasets (CheXpert and ChestMNIST), against seven federated baselines under identical local training settings. FedDRAW achieved the highest average rank among all eight methods under both AUC and the geometric mean (GM) of sensitivity and specificity, which a Friedman test with Nemenyi post-hoc analysis confirmed to be a statistically significant difference between the methods. Scheduling two signals, rather than fixing the weights by sample count alone, could enable less biased diagnostic models.

</details>


### [320] [Hessian-based molecular conformation augmentation for a scalable and efficient strategy of machine learning interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05233)
*Bumju Kwak,Jeonghee Jo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两种基于Hessian的数据增强方案（UniAug和ModeAug），通过简单的泰勒展开生成位移数据，在不改变训练目标和扩展自动微分图的情况下，将Hessian信息隐式融入机器学习原子间势的建模，提升模型精度且即插即用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLIP已能较好学习势能面和原子力，但振动分析、过渡态搜索等实际应用高度依赖Hessian信息。标准MLIP仅用能量和力训练，未利用Hessian；而现有显式纳入Hessian的方法需要修改架构且因高阶反向传播带来显著计算和内存开销。

Method: 提出两种基于Hessian推导的数据增强方案：(1) 各向同性高斯位移（UniAug）；(2) 简正模式加权位移（ModeAug）。两者利用泰勒展开近似构造位移样本，无需改变训练目标函数或延长自动微分图，可无缝即插即用集成到现有架构和训练流程中。

Result: 在非平衡与平衡数据集上的综合评估表明，所提方法能提升模型精度，并提供具有实用价值的任务特定指导原则。

Conclusion: 基于Hessian的数据增强策略在不增加训练目标和架构改动的前提下，有效利用Hessian信息提升MLIP性能，为需要高精度Hessian的应用场景提供了轻量级且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: While machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have successfully learned potential energy surfaces (PES) and atomic forces, many practical applications, such as vibrational analysis and transition state search, rely heavily on the PES Hessian. Yet, standard MLIPs tend to be trained on energy and forces alone, leaving Hessian information largely unexploited. Meanwhile, existing methods that explicitly incorporate the Hessian into training objectives require architectural modifications and introduce significant computational and memory overheads due to higher-order backpropagation. To address these limitations, we propose two Hessian-derived data augmentation schemes: isotropic Gaussian displacement (\textbf{UniAug}) and normal mode-weighted displacement (\textbf{ModeAug}). Both methods utilize simple Taylor expansions, achieving effective augmentation without altering training objectives or extending the autograd graph. This allows seamless, plug-and-play integration with existing architectures and training pipelines. Comprehensive evaluations across non-equilibrium and equilibrium datasets demonstrate that our approach enhances model accuracy while providing practical, task-specific guidelines.

</details>


### [321] [PRICE: A Systematic Study of LLM Adaptation Choices for Bitcoin Price Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05235)
*Maryam Fakhari,Mehran Safayani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRICE框架，系统研究LLM在短期比特币价格预测中的适应性选择，基于4-bit量化的LLaMA-3 8B模型，整合LoRA微调、递归多步推理、整数值表示、CTF提示和零温度解码，在多个基线上实现最低预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密货币市场具有极端波动性和非平稳动态特征，传统预测方法面临挑战。虽然LLM在时间序列预测中展现出潜力，但在金融环境中，不同适应性选择（如微调、数值表示、提示策略、推理和解码方式）的组合效应尚未被充分探索。

Method: PRICE框架包含五个关键组件：(1) 使用LoRA进行参数高效微调，在有限硬件上实现高效训练；(2) 递归多步推理以改进预测精度；(3) 整数值舍入的数值表示以减少误差；(4) 上下文-任务-格式(CTF)提示策略；(5) 精确零温度解码以提升递归预测的稳定性。基于4-bit量化的LLaMA-3 8B模型构建。

Result: 消融实验表明每个组件均对预测准确性和可靠性有贡献。与八个基于Transformer和时间序列基础模型相比，PRICE在验证集和测试集上均取得最低预测误差，并在不同评估期间保持稳健性能。尽管基于主要预训练于文本而非时间序列数据的模型，PRICE仍优于或媲美专门的基础模型。CTF提示优于CoT、iCoT和few-shot提示。

Conclusion: 适应性选择（包括微调策略、数值表示、提示设计、推理方式和解码设置）对LLM在数值时间序列预测中的准确性和鲁棒性起决定性作用。PRICE证明了通过系统化的适应性设计，文本预训练的LLM也能在金融时间序列预测中达到竞争优势。

Abstract: Cryptocurrency markets exhibit extreme volatility and non-stationary dynamics that challenge conventional forecasting methods. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promise for time series forecasting, the combined effects of adaptation choices remain largely unexplored in financial settings. This study introduces PRICE, a structured approach for adapting LLMs to short-term Bitcoin price forecasting. Built on a 4-bit quantized LLaMA-3 8B model, PRICE investigates how fine-tuning, numerical representation, prompting, inference, and decoding jointly influence forecasting performance. PRICE integrates Parameter-efficient fine-tuning with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), Recursive multi-step inference, Integer-rounded numerical representation, Context-Task-Format (CTF) prompting, and Exact zero-temperature decoding. Ablation studies show that each component contributes to forecasting accuracy and reliability. LoRA enables efficient training on limited hardware, recursive inference improves accuracy, integer-rounded values reduce errors, CTF prompting outperforms Chain-of-Thought, Implicit Chain-of-Thought (iCoT), and few-shot prompting, and zero-temperature decoding improves stability during recursive forecasting. Comparative evaluation against eight transformer-based and time-series foundation models shows that PRICE achieves the lowest forecasting errors on both validation and test sets while maintaining robust performance across evaluation periods. Despite being based on a model primarily pretrained on text rather than time-series data, PRICE achieves competitive or superior performance relative to specialized foundation models. These findings demonstrate that adaptation choices critically determine the accuracy and robustness of LLMs for numerical time-series forecasting.

</details>


### [322] [GLASS: Graph-Language Alignment with Spherical Scoring for Transferable Graph-Level Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05253)
*Xudong Wang,Chris Ding,Tongxin Li,Jicong Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GLASS是一个用于图级异常检测（GLAD）的统一框架，通过在单位超球面上进行图-语言对齐来实现跨域可迁移的异常检测。它利用Graph Descriptor Prompt (GraphDP)将图的局部、全局和语义属性序列化为文本桥接，通过Matryoshka表示切片保证多尺度一致性，并使用Spherical Multi-Modal Scoring (SMS)在球面上进行基于von Mises-Fisher核密度估计的异常打分，支持零样本和少样本跨域迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图级异常检测方法通常局限于单一领域，缺乏跨域可迁移性；同时需要在不同领域/分布下稳健地检测图结构中的异常，这要求模型能够对齐结构信息与语义信息，建立统一的可迁移表示空间。

Method: 1) 通过多切片软余弦目标，将结构感知的图编码器与指令感知的文本嵌入对齐到单位超球面上，构建统一表示空间；2) 设计Graph Descriptor Prompt (GraphDP)，将图的局部、全局和语义属性序列化为紧凑的文本桥接；3) 采用Matryoshka表示切片强制多尺度一致性，捕捉不同粒度的异常偏差；4) 提出Spherical Multi-Modal Scoring (SMS)，在图和文本嵌入空间中实例化von Mises-Fisher核密度估计器进行异常打分，其概率公式在高浓度极限下能恢复角度k近邻打分，并对结构性和语义性异常信号进行融合。

Result: 在12个基准和3个元领域中，GLASS在平均AUROC和排名上均优于现有的高级GLAD基线，并实现了有效的跨域迁移，包括零样本（无需目标域训练数据）和少样本（仅需少量正常样本参考集校准）场景下的异常检测。

Conclusion: 通过在超球面上实现图与语言的统一对齐，以及基于概率密度估计的多模态打分机制，GLASS成功实现了跨域可迁移的图级异常检测，为GLAD提供了领域无关的通用解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce GLASS, a framework for graph-level anomaly detection (GLAD) that achieves robust cross-domain transferability through graph-language alignment on the unit hypersphere. GLASS builds a unified representation space by aligning a structure-aware graph encoder with an instruction-aware text embedding via a multi-slice soft cosine objective. Our framework serializes local, global, and semantic graph properties into a compact Graph Descriptor Prompt (GraphDP), creating a text bridge that enables domain-agnostic anomaly scoring. By enforcing multi-scale consistency through Matryoshka representation slices, the model captures anomalous deviations at multiple levels of granularity. For scoring, we formulate anomaly detection as density estimation on the aligned hypersphere and introduce Spherical Multi-Modal Scoring (SMS), which instantiates von Mises-Fisher kernel density estimators in both graph and text embedding spaces. This probabilistic formulation recovers angular k-nearest-neighbor scoring as a high-concentration limiting case and provides a principled fusion of structural and semantic anomaly signals. The shared text embedding space further serves as a cross-domain bridge: by encoding a target domain's GraphDP without target-domain training data, GLASS performs zero-shot anomaly detection, and with only a handful of normal examples, few-shot adaptation via reference-set calibration. Across twelve benchmarks and three meta-domains, GLASS obtains the best average AUROC and rank compared with recent advanced GLAD baselines and enables effective cross-domain transfer.

</details>


### [323] [How to Speculate about Uncertainty in Agentic Coding? A Draft-Model Gate Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05274)
*Konstantin Grotov,Valentin Malykh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Speculative Uncertainty (SU)方法，通过小型开源草稿模型对黑盒LLM智能体已生成的轨迹进行单次前向评分，仅从输出token中恢复预测性失败信号，无需访问logits、权重或重复采样，可用于路由、人工干预或额外测试时计算等下游策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署于软件工程场景的LLM智能体失败成本高昂：它们会自信地犯错，但错误的动作往往只有在昂贵的执行和重试之后才能被发现。现有方法缺乏对黑盒智能体输出的廉、可用的失败预测信号。

Method: SU方法反转了推测解码（speculative decoding）的思路：用一个小型开放权重草稿模型对智能体已生成的轨迹进行单次前向打分，得到推测性交叉似然（speculative cross-likelihoods）。通过分离推理与动作片段来提取阶段感知特征（phase-aware features），并针对可验证的目标进行校准，最终输出一个失败似然分数。

Result: 在Qwen3-Coder-480B和Claude 3.5 Sonnet两个软件工程智能体上实例化了执行前否决门（pre-execution veto gate）策略，将执行错误率降低6-8个百分点，token成本降低14-19%。该方法可无需重训练迁移到分布外基准，并跨智能体模型泛化。

Conclusion: SU从黑盒LLM智能体的输出token中即可提取可操作的失败预测信号，无需内部访问或重复采样，且该信号能被下游策略直接消费，在真实部署中显著降低了执行错误率和token成本。

Abstract: LLM agents deployed for software engineering fail expensively: they act confidently wrong, and bad actions are recognized only after costly execution and retry. We present Speculative Uncertainty (SU), a method that recovers a predictive failure signal for a black-box agent from its output tokens alone, with no access to logits, weights, activations, or repeated sampling. Inverting speculative decoding, a small open-weight draft model scores the agent's already-generated trajectory in a single forward pass. From these speculative cross-likelihoods we extract phase-aware features by separating the reasoning and action spans, and calibrate them against a verifiable objective. SU produces a failure-likelihood score that any downstream policy, such as routing, human intervention, or extra test-time compute, can consume directly. To show the signal is actionable, we instantiate one such policy, a pre-execution veto gate, on software engineering agents Qwen3-Coder-480B and closed-source Claude 3.5 Sonnet, cutting execution error rate by 6-8 percentage points and token cost by 14-19% in deployment, transferring to out-of-distribution benchmarks without retraining, and generalizing across agent models.

</details>


### [324] [Learning from VAE Errors to support ECG-based Differential Diagnosis of Myocardial Scar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05294)
*Shayan Sharifi,Riccardo Treu,Ilaria Gandin,Federico Garoia,Marco Merlo,Giulia Cisotto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估了基于β-变分自编码器（β-VAE）的ECG表示能否区分心肌病患者的LGE+（晚期钆增强阳性，即存在心肌瘢痕）与LGE-状态。在300名受试者的本地队列中，对比了基础ECGx.AI模型的32维特征与在正常PTB-XL ECGs上训练的浅层β-VAE特征。ECGx.AI配合随机森林达到AUC 0.686，β-VAE配合梯度提升达到AUC 0.577（灵敏度0.775）。值得注意的是，12个导联中有10个的DTW重建误差在两类间存在显著差异，配合逻辑回归达到AUC 0.643，表明DTW重建误差可作为瘢痕相关ECG改变的潜在标志物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LGE（晚期钆增强）心脏磁共振是心肌瘢痕的关键标志，但其可及性有限。该研究旨在探索基于常规ECG的筛查方法，利用β-VAE衍生的ECG表示来判别心肌瘢痕，以弥补LGE-MRI在临床可及性方面的不足。

Method: 研究对比了两类特征：(1) 基础模型ECGx.AI提取的32维特征；(2) 在正常PTB-XL ECGs上训练的浅层β-VAE特征。采用下游分类（随机森林、梯度提升、逻辑回归）及基于动态时间规整（DTW）的重建误差进行评估，使用Mann-Whitney U检验比较两类间的DTW重建误差差异。

Result: ECGx.AI配合随机森林达到AUC 0.686；β-VAE配合梯度提升达到AUC 0.577（灵敏度0.775）；DTW重建误差在12个导联中有10个在LGE+与LGE-之间存在显著差异，配合逻辑回归达到AUC 0.643。

Conclusion: DTW重建误差在心肌瘢痕相关的ECG改变检测中展现出作为标志物的潜力，其分类性能优于β-VAE原始特征且具有在多数导联上的显著判别力，支持进一步探索基于重建误差的ECG筛查方法。

Abstract: Late Gadolinium Enhancement (LGE) on cardiac magnetic resonance is a key marker of myocardial scar, but its limited accessibility motivates routine ECG-based screening. We evaluated whether $β$-variational autoencoder (VAE)-derived ECG representations can discriminate LGE+ from LGE- cardiomyopathic patients in a local cohort of 300 subjects. We compared 32-dimensional features from the foundation ECGx.AI model with those from a shallower $β$-VAE trained on normal PTB-XL ECGs, evaluating downstream classification and Dynamic Time Warping (DTW)-based reconstruction errors. ECGx.AI reached an area under ROC of 0.686 with Random Forest, while the proposed $β$-VAE reached 0.577 with sensitivity of 0.775 with Gradient Boosting. Notably, DTW-reconstruction errors significantly differed between classes in 10 out of 12 leads according to Mann-Whitney U test and help in classification, leading to an area under ROC of 0.643 with Logistic Regression, supporting their potential as markers of scar-related ECG alterations.

</details>


### [325] [How Does mHC Use Its Residual Streams? Selective Routing and Near-Identity Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05309)
*Pengxiang Zhao,Xing Li,Xianzhi Yu,Wei Guo,Zhenhua Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过有效流数、跨流残差权重和余弦相似度等指标，系统分析了DeepSeek-V4-Flash中四流残差路径的使用模式，发现读写路由集中在约两个流上且随深度变化，残差混合主要发生在浅层，并通过定向干预（替换晚期mixers、固定早期mixers、裁剪路由权重）验证了这些模式的因果重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超连接(Hyper-Connections)及其流形约束变体mHC将残差通路从一个流扩展到n个流，但训练后的模型如何使用这一表达能力尚不明确——块级读写广度、残差通路对流间的混合程度以及各流是否携带不同的表征，这些问题此前缺乏系统的实证分析。

Method: 以DeepSeek-V4-Flash的四流残差路径为研究对象，采用三类指标：(1)有效流数衡量块级读写集中度；(2)跨流残差权重衡量流间混合强度；(3)层间余弦相似度衡量表征区分度。并进行三类干预实验：将晚期mixers替换为单位映射、将早期mixers固定为其C4诊断均值、每站点仅保留token级别的三大路由权重，分别测其对C4困惑度和六任务平均分的影响。

Result: 读写路由集中但随深度变化，典型注意力或FFN位点实际约使用两个流，主导流随层变化且表征方向性保持区分；残差混合温和且主要在浅层，22-42层路径基本各流独立前传。干预结果显示：晚期mixers替换为单位映射仅使困惑度增加1.9%且六任务平均分不变；早期mixers被替换则困惑度增加41%；将早期mixers固定为C4诊断均值困惑度仅增0.2%且平均分降0.25个百分点；每站点保留三大路由权重困惑度最多增加2.7%。

Conclusion: 该模型仅实现了四流mHC所提供的一部分灵活性：单个块很少需要全部四个流，晚期残差混合带来的可测收益甚微；早期mixers的站点特异性结构比其token级变化更为关键。这一发现对超连接架构的能力-使用错配问题提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Hyper-Connections and their manifold-constrained variant mHC widen a residual pathway from one stream to n, yet how trained models use this capacity remains unclear: how broadly blocks read and write, how strongly the residual pathway mixes streams, and whether the streams carry distinct representations. We examine these properties in the four-stream residual pathway of DeepSeek-V4-Flash using effective stream counts, cross-stream residual weights, and inter-stream cosine similarity. Read/write routing is concentrated but varies across depth: a typical attention or FFN site effectively uses about two streams, while the dominant stream changes across layers and the representations remain directionally distinct. Residual mixing is modest and occurs primarily in early layers; in layers 22-42, the pathway mostly carries each stream forward separately. Targeted interventions establish the functional significance of these patterns. Replacing the late mixers by identity increases C4 perplexity by only 1.9% and preserves the six-task average score, whereas replacing the early mixers increases perplexity by 41%. Fixing each early mixer to its C4 diagnostic mean increases perplexity by only 0.2% and reduces the average score by 0.25 percentage points, showing that its site-specific structure matters more than its token-wise variation on the evaluated metrics.
